Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "ARANACAKDEYIM","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.ARANACAKDEYIM=""

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STACK","IO")

	MTableT.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "URUNAGACI", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MPSNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "GRUPEVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "FOKOD", "CHAR",6,0,"L","","F2=DO_BRW('FOSYSFT')"
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SF_SF_UNIT", "CHAR",6,0,"L","","STUNIT"
	MTableT.Addfield2 "REV_RTESTARIH", "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "USTSEVIYE_KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "USTSEVIYE_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "ANAMPS", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KAYNAKMPS", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SIPNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SIPARTNO", "CHAR",16,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "CH_KODU", "CHAR",16,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('CARI00')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "CH_ADI", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "D_ID", "CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "XLEVEL", "DOUBLE",2,0,"R","",""

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub MPSHesapla()
'Sub Makro1()
	Set MTable=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")	
	Set ST40E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set M10E = GetAllLevels()

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT.Empty

	Dim i 

	For i=1 to M10E.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Ekrana Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & M10E.GetRowCount()
		StokKodu = M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(i)
		MTableT.AddRow
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(i)
		MTableT.SELECTED="E"
		MTableT.URUNAGACI=GetUrunAgaci(StokKodu)
		MTableT.KOD=StokKodu
		MTableT.AD= Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
		MTableT.FOKOD=M10E.FOKOD_MAMULKODU(i)
		MTableT.SF_MIKTAR=M10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI(i)
		MTableT.SF_SF_UNIT = M10E.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		MTableT.REV_RTESTARIH = M10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH(i)
		MTableT.SIPARTNO = M10E.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(i)
		MPSInFo = GetMPSInfo(StokKodu,M10E.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(i))
		MTableT.MPSNO = MPSInFo(0)
		MTableT.GRUPEVRAKNO = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "GRUPEVRAKNO", MPSInFo(0), "")
		MTableT.ANAMPS = MPSInFo(1)
		MTableT.KAYNAKMPS = MPSInFo(2)
		if trim(MTableT.MPSNO(i)) = "" and trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) <> "" Then
			if trim(M10E.EVRAKNO(i)) <> "" Then MTableT.MPSNO = "YUrt." & trim(M10E.EVRAKNO(i))
			if trim(M10E.ANAMPS(i)) <> "" Then MTableT.ANAMPS = "YUrt." & trim(M10E.ANAMPS(i))
			if trim(M10E.KAYNAKMPS(i)) <> "" Then MTableT.KAYNAKMPS = "YUrt." & trim(M10E.KAYNAKMPS(i))
			MTableT.XLEVEL = M10E.XLEVEL(i)
			MTableT.GRUPEVRAKNO = M10E.GRUPEVRAKNO(i)
		End if
		MTableT.USTSEVIYE_KOD = M10E.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)
		MTableT.USTSEVIYE_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10E.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i), "")
		'MTableT.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = M10E.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)

		if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
			MTableT.SIPNO = ST40E.EVRAKNO
			MTableT.CH_KODU = ST40E.MUSTERIKODU
			MTableT.CH_ADI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", ST40E.MUSTERIKODU, "")
			MTableT.D_ID = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(StokKodu, ST40E.MUSTERIKODU)
		End if
	Next
	
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "__MAK111"
End Sub
Sub MPSOlustur()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
	Set MMPS10E = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set MMPS10TM = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")

	Set MTable=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")	

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	For i=1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage i & " / " & MTableT.GetRecCount()
		StokKodu = MTableT.KOD(i)
		if trim(StokKodu) <> "" and MTableT.SELECTED(i) = "E" and MTableT.URUNAGACI(i) = "E" _
				   and (trim(MTableT.MPSNO(i)) = "" or InStr(1,MTableT.MPSNO(i), "YUrt.") > 0) Then
			nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()

			MMPS10E.HAVUZKODU="LP"
			MMPS10E.STATUS="P"
			MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU=MTableT.KOD(i)
			MMPS10E.GRUPEVRAKNO=MTableT.GRUPEVRAKNO(i)
			MMPS10E.FOKOD_MAMULKODU=MTableT.FOKOD(i)
			MMPS10E.SF_PAKETSAYISI = 1
			MMPS10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			MMPS10E.SF_SF_UNIT = MTableT.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
			MMPS10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH = MTableT.REV_RTESTARIH(i)	
'			MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1 = trim(MTableT.USTSEVIYE_KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.USTSEVIYE_AD(i))
			AnaMPSKod = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "MAMULSTOKKODU", MTableT.ANAMPS(i), "")
			AnaMPSAd = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", AnaMPSKod, "")
'			MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1 = trim(AnaMPSKod) & " - " & trim(AnaMPSAd)
			MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1 = trim(AnaMPSKod)
			MMPS10E.NOTLAR_2 = MTableT.D_ID(i)
			MMPS10E.ANAMPS= MTableT.ANAMPS(i)
			MMPS10E.KAYNAKMPS = MTableT.KAYNAKMPS(i)
			'MMPS10E.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)

			nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
			For j = 1 to MMPS10TM.GetRowCount()
				MMPS10TM.MUSTERISIPINO(j) = MTableT.SIPNO(i)
				MMPS10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(j) = MTableT.SIPARTNO(i)
				MMPS10TM.MUSTERIKODU(j) = MTableT.CH_KODU(i)
				'MMPS10TM.SF_VRI_VR_R1(j) = MTableT.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
			Next
			nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
			MTableT.SetCurrentRow(i)
			EskiMPS = MTableT.MPSNO(i)
			MTableT.MPSNO(i) = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO
			Call YUrtGuncelle(i, EskiMPS, MTableT.MPSNO(i))
		End if
	Next
	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()

	Call AltSeviyeRTTGuncelle()

	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub SecIptal()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	if MTableT.SELECTED(1) = "E" Then
		YeniSecim = "H"
	Else
		YeniSecim = "E"
	End if
	AktifSatir = MTableT.GetCurrentRow()
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(i)
		MTableT.SELECTED(i) = YeniSecim
	Next
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(AktifSatir)
End Sub
Function GetAllLevels()
	On Error Resume Next
	Dim LevelMPSNo(99)
	Dim Seviye
	Seviye = 0
'	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=MMPS10E", "REPORT"
'	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	Set M10E = CreateMPStable()

	Set ST40T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	ST40T.SafePack

	MPSSiraNo = 0
	For i = 1 To ST40T.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS 'ler hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & ST40T.GetRowCount()
		M10E.AddRow
		if i <> 1 then
			M10E.AddRow
		end if
		satirNo = M10E.GetRecCount
		MPSSiraNo = MPSSiraNo + 1
		Seviye = 0
		M10E.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(satirNo) = ST40T.KOD(i)
		M10E.FOKOD_MAMULKODU(satirNo) = ST40T.FOKOD(i)
		M10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI(satirNo) = ST40T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		M10E.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = ST40T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		M10E.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(satirNo) = ST40T.ARTNO(i)
		M10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40T.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
'		M10E.SF_VRI_VR_R1(satirNo) = ST40T.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
		AnaMpsNo = MPSSiraNo
		LevelMPSNo(Seviye) = AnaMpsNo
		M10E.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = AnaMpsNo

		StokKodu = ST40T.KOD(i)
		Miktar = ST40T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MusteriSipArtNo = ST40T.ARTNO(i)
		TeslimTarihi = ST40T.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
		FOKodu = ST40T.FOKOD(i)

		res = Doc.Calc_Bom("M","M",StokKodu,1,Miktar,"", FOKodu,"","","","","","")
		Set Tbmrsys = Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT")

'		Tbmrsys.GroupBy("BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE")
'		Tbmrsys.Sort("XLEVEL+BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE")
		For j = 1 To Tbmrsys.GetRecCount()
			If Doc.Is_EY1(Tbmrsys.IALTSEVIYEBULUNDU(j)) Then
				M10E.AddRow
				MPSSiraNo = MPSSiraNo + 1
				satirNo = M10E.GetRecCount()
				M10E.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j)
				M10E.FOKOD_MAMULKODU(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.FOKOD(j)
				M10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.KAYNAKMIKTART(j)
				M10E.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j), "")
				M10E.SF_VRI_VR_R3(satirNo) = GetUstSeviyeKod(Tbmrsys, j)
				M10E.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(satirNo) = MusteriSipArtNo
				M10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH(satirNo) = TeslimTarihi
				'M10E.SFTT(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.XLEVEL(j)
				M10E.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = MPSSiraNo
				M10E.ANAMPS(satirNo) = AnaMpsNo
				Seviye = CInt(trim(Tbmrsys.XLEVEL(j)))
				LevelMPSNo(Seviye) = MPSSiraNo
				M10E.KAYNAKMPS(satirNo) = LevelMPSNo(Seviye-1) 'KaynakMpsNo
				EskiLevel = Tbmrsys.XLEVEL(j)
				M10E.XLEVEL(satirNo) = Doc.CDbl(Tbmrsys.XLEVEL(j)) 
				EskiMPSSiraNo = MPSSiraNo
			End If
		Next
	Next
	Set GetAllLevels = M10E
End Function
Function GetUstSeviyeKod(Tbmrsys, satirNo)
	Seviye = Doc.Cdbl(Tbmrsys.XLEVEL(satirNo))
	if Seviye = 1 Then 
		GetUstSeviyeKod = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_CODE(1)
		Exit Function
	End If
	for i = satirNo - 1 to 1 Step -1
		if Doc.Cdbl(Tbmrsys.XLEVEL(i)) = Seviye - 1 Then
			GetUstSeviyeKod = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function GetUrunAgaci(StokKodu)
	FoyNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("BOMU01E", "EVRAKNO", StokKodu + "*", "MAMULCODE+AKTIF_PASIF")
	if trim(FoyNo) <> "" Then
		GetUrunAgaci = "E"
	Else
		GetUrunAgaci = "H"
	End if
End Function
Function GetMPSInfo(StokKodu, SipArtNo)
	Dim MPSArr(2)
	MPSArr(0) = ""
	MPSArr(1) = ""
	MPSArr(2) = ""
	Set MPS10TM = Doc.Select1("ABC", "MMPS10TM", "MUSTERISIPIARTNO='" & SipArtNo & "'&MAMULSTOKKODU='" & StokKodu & "'","",1)
	if MPS10TM.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		MPSArr(0) = MPS10TM.EVRAKNO(1)
		MPSArr(1) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "ANAMPS", MPS10TM.EVRAKNO(1), "")
		MPSArr(2) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "KAYNAKMPS", MPS10TM.EVRAKNO(1), "")
	End if
	GetMPSInfo = MPSArr
End Function
Sub YUrtGuncelle(satirNo, EskiMPS, YeniMPS)
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	For i = satirNo to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if trim(MTableT.ANAMPS(i)) = trim(EskiMPS) Then MTableT.ANAMPS(i) = YeniMPS
		if trim(MTableT.KAYNAKMPS(i)) = trim(EskiMPS) Then MTableT.KAYNAKMPS(i) = YeniMPS
	Next
End Sub
Sub AltSeviyeRTTGuncelle()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
	Set M10E = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	For j = 1 to 15
		For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
			if MTableT.XLEVEL(i) = j Then 
				QStr = "EVRAKNO='"+MTableT.KAYNAKMPS(i)+"'&R_KAYNAKKODU='"+MTableT.KOD(i)+"'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'"
				Set M10T = Doc.Select1("ddd", "MMPS10T", QStr, "", -1)
				if M10T.GetRowCount > 0 Then
					Doc1.Load_Voucher MTableT.MPSNO(i)
					M10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH = M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH
					MTableT.REV_RTESTARIH(i) = M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH
					Doc1.Save_Voucher
				End if
			End if
		Next
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Function CreateMPSTable()
	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("xxx=STACK","IO")

	MTableT.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "GRUPEVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MAMULSTOKKODU", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "FOKOD_MAMULKODU", "CHAR",6,0,"L","","F2=DO_BRW('FOSYSFT')"
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI", "DOUBLE",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SF_SF_UNIT", "CHAR",6,0,"L","","STUNIT"
	MTableT.Addfield2 "URETIMDENTESTARIH", "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SF_VRI_VR_R3", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "ANAMPS", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KAYNAKMPS", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "MUSTERISIPIARTNO", "CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "XLEVEL", "DOUBLE",2,0,"R","",""
	Set CreateMPSTable = MTableT
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MPS-GUN1
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : MPS Miktarlarýný Ürün Aðacýna Göre Güncelle (Alt MPS 'lerle birlikte)
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	EvrakTipi = "UP"
	MPSNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO
	TRNum = ""
	
	res = MPSHesapla()

	EvrakNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO
	Doc.Save_Voucher()

	Set ML1 = Doc.RunMacro3("MAKR7S.BAGLANTI", "GetBaglantiliMPSList1", EvrakTipi, MPSNo, TrNum)
	if ML1.GetRowCount() < 2 Then Exit Sub
	For i = 2 to ML1.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Baðlantýlý MPS 'ler Güncelleniyor, MPS No : " & ML1.EVRAKNO(i) & ", " & (i-1) & " / " & (ML1.GetRowCount()-1)
		Doc.Load_Voucher ML1.EVRAKNO(i)
		Degisti = False
		For j = 1 to MMPS10TM.GetRowCount()
			if MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(j) = "UP" Then
				IhtiyacMiktari = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_MIKTART", MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS(j) & MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(j), "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
				if MMPS10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(j) <> IhtiyacMiktari Then
					MMPS10TM.SetCurrentRow(j)
					MMPS10TM.SF_PAKETICERIGI(j) = Doc.Div(IhtiyacMiktari, MMPS10TM.SF_PAKETSAYISI(j))
					Degisti = True
				End if
			End if
		Next
		if Degisti Then
			Set M10TM2 = Doc.Select2G("dw3fwfsf", "MMPS10TM", "", "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
			EskiMiktar = MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR
			MMPS10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI = Doc.Div(M10TM2.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1), MMPS10E.SF_PAKETSAYISI)
			For j = 1 to MMPS10T.GetRowCount()
				MMPS10T.SetCurrentRow(j)
				MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0 = Doc.Div(M10TM2.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1) * MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0, EskiMiktar)
				MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI = Doc.Div(M10TM2.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1) * MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI, EskiMiktar)
			Next
			Doc.Save_Voucher
		End if
	Next
	Doc.Load_Voucher EvrakNo
End Sub
Function MPSHesapla()
	MPSHesapla = False
	MMPS10T.SafePack()
	ret = Doc.Calc_Bom("1","M", MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU,MMPS10E.SF_PAKETSAYISI,MMPS10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI,"","","","","","","","")  	 	
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT") 		

	if not AyniMi(Tbmrsys) Then Exit Function
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		MMPS10T.SetCurrentRow(i)
		if Doc.Round(MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0,3) <> Doc.Round(Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i),3) Then
			MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0 = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i) 
			MPSHesapla = True
		End if
		if MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR1 <> Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(i) Then
			MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR1 = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(i) 
			MPSHesapla = True
		End if
		if MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKET <> Tbmrsys.BOMREC_YMAMULPS(i) Then
			MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKET = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_YMAMULPS(i)
			MPSHesapla = True
		End if
		if MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI <> Tbmrsys.BOMREC_YMAMULPM(i) Then
			MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_YMAMULPM(i)
			MPSHesapla = True
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function AyniMi(Tbmrsys)
	Set M10T = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
	Set M10T2 = Doc.Select2("qwd32dwfasf", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKKODU>='110 R'&R_KAYNAKKODU<='110 RZZZZ'", "", -1)
	AyniMi = True
	if Tbmrsys.GetRowCount() <> (M10T.GetRowCount() - M10T2.GetRowCount()) Then
		AyniMi = False
		Exit Function
	End if
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) <> M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i) or Tbmrsys.BOMREC_SIRANO(i) <> M10T.R_SIRANO(i) Then
			AyniMi = False
			Exit Function	
		Else
			if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) <> M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) Then
				if trim(M10T.ALTERNATIFKODU(i)) = "" Then
					AyniMi = False
					Exit Function	
				Else	
					QStr = "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&BOMREC_CODE='" & M10T.R_MAMULKODU(i) & "'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='" & _
							M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i) & "'&BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='" & M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) & _
							"'&BOMREC_ALTKODU='" & M10T.ALTERNATIFKODU(i) & "'"
					Set B01T = Doc.Select1("daefd3fff", "BOMU01T", QStr, "SRNUM", -1)
					if B01T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
						AyniMi = False
						Exit Function	
					Else
						Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i) = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(1), B01T.BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR(1)) * MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, 6)
						Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(i) = B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(1)
					End if
				End if
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MPS-GUN2
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Üzerinde Bulunulan MPS 'in miktarýný Sipariþlerden Tekrar Hesapla, Baðlantýlý MPS 'leri Güncelle
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set M10T = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
	Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")

	Degisti = False
	For i = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		if M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(i) = "SP" Then
			Set S40T = Doc.Select1("df23fsdf43f", "STOK40T", "ARTNO='" & M10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			ToleransliSipMiktari = GetToleransliSipMiktar(S40T, M10TM, i)
			if M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i) <> ToleransliSipMiktari Then
				M10TM.SetCurrentRow(i)
				M10TM.SF_PAKETSAYISI = S40T.SF_PMIKTAR
				M10TM.SF_PAKETICERIGI = Doc.Div(ToleransliSipMiktari, M10TM.SF_PAKETSAYISI)
				Degisti = True
			End if
		End if
	Next

	if Not Degisti Then Exit Sub
	Call MMPS10TMyeGoreMPSHesapla()

	EvrakTipi = "UP"
	MPSNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO
	TRNum = ""
	
	Set ML1 = Doc.RunMacro3("MAKR7S.BAGLANTI", "GetBaglantiliMPSList1", EvrakTipi, MPSNo, TrNum)
	if ML1.GetRowCount() < 2 Then Exit Sub
	For i = 2 to ML1.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Baðlantýlý MPS 'ler Güncelleniyor, MPS No : " & ML1.EVRAKNO(i) & ", " & (i-1) & " / " & (ML1.GetRowCount()-1)
		Doc.Load_Voucher ML1.EVRAKNO(i)
		Degisti = False
		For j = 1 to MMPS10TM.GetRowCount()
			if MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(j) = "UP" Then
				IhtiyacMiktari = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_MIKTART", MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS(j) & MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(j), "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
				if MMPS10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(j) <> IhtiyacMiktari Then
					MMPS10TM.SetCurrentRow(j)
					MMPS10TM.SF_PAKETICERIGI(j) = Doc.Div(IhtiyacMiktari, MMPS10TM.SF_PAKETSAYISI(j))
					Degisti = True
				End if
			End if
		Next
		if Degisti Then
			Set M10TM2 = Doc.Select2G("dw3fwfsf", "MMPS10TM", "", "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
			EskiMiktar = MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR
			MMPS10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI = Doc.Div(M10TM2.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1), MMPS10E.SF_PAKETSAYISI)
			For j = 1 to MMPS10T.GetRowCount()
				MMPS10T.SetCurrentRow(j)
				MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0 = Doc.Div(M10TM2.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1) * MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0, EskiMiktar)
				MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI = Doc.Div(M10TM2.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1) * MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI, EskiMiktar)
			Next
			Doc.Save_Voucher
		End if
	Next
	Doc.Load_Voucher EvrakNo
End Sub
Function AyniMi(Tbmrsys)
	Set M10T = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
	Set M10T2 = Doc.Select2("qwd32dwfasf", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKKODU>='110 R'&R_KAYNAKKODU<='110 RZZZZ'", "", -1)
	AyniMi = True
	if Tbmrsys.GetRowCount() <> (M10T.GetRowCount() - M10T2.GetRowCount()) Then
		AyniMi = False
		Exit Function
	End if
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) <> M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i) or Tbmrsys.BOMREC_SIRANO(i) <> M10T.R_SIRANO(i) Then
			AyniMi = False
			Exit Function	
		Else
			if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) <> M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) Then
				if trim(M10T.ALTERNATIFKODU(i)) = "" Then
					AyniMi = False
					Exit Function	
				Else	
					QStr = "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&BOMREC_CODE='" & M10T.R_MAMULKODU(i) & "'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='" & _
							M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i) & "'&BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='" & M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) & _
							"'&BOMREC_ALTKODU='" & M10T.ALTERNATIFKODU(i) & "'"
					Set B01T = Doc.Select1("daefd3fff", "BOMU01T", QStr, "SRNUM", -1)
					if B01T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
						AyniMi = False
						Exit Function	
					Else
						Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i) = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(1), B01T.BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR(1)) * MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, 6)
						Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(i) = B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(1)
					End if
				End if
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function
Sub MMPS10TMyeGoreMPSHesapla()
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set M10T = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
	Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	EskiMiktar = M10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR

	Set M10TM1 = Doc.Select2("w4fsfsdf3wfe", "MMPS10TM", "", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to M10TM1.GetRowCount()
		M10TM1.NOTLAR_1(i) = M10TM.SF_PAKETSAYISI(i)
	Next
	M10TM1.GroupBy("EVRAKNO")
	if Doc.CDbl(trim(M10TM1.SF_PAKETSAYISI)) = M10TM.SF_PAKETSAYISI Then
		M10E.SF_PAKETSAYISI = M10TM.SF_PAKETSAYISI
	Else
		M10E.SF_PAKETSAYISI = 1
	End if
	M10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI  = Doc.Div(M10TM1.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, M10E.SF_PAKETSAYISI)
	Oran = Doc.Div(M10TM1.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, EskiMiktar)

	For j = 1 to MMPS10T.GetRowCount()
		M10T.SetCurrentRow(j)
		M10T.R_MIKTAR0 = M10T.R_MIKTAR0 * Oran
		M10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI = M10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI * Oran
		M10T.R_MIKTART_ALT = M10T.R_MIKTART_ALT * Oran
	Next
	Doc.Save_Voucher
End Sub
Function GetToleransliSipMiktar(S40T, M10TM, satirNo)
	ToleransTuru = S40T.TOLERANSTYPE
	Tolerans = S40T.TOLERANS
	GetToleransliSipMiktar = S40T.SF_MIKTAR
	if trim(ToleransTuru) = "+" or trim(ToleransTuru) = "-+" Then
		GetToleransliSipMiktar = Doc.Round(S40T.SF_MIKTAR * (1 + Tolerans / 100),0)
	End if	
End Function

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MPSDEGISTR
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Üzerinde Bulunulan MPS 'i Ýptal Et, Baðlantýlý MPS 'leri Güncelle
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	'Set ML = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM_BAGLANTILI_LISTE")
	EvrakTuru = "UP"
	EvrakNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO
	TRNum = MMPS10TM.TRNUM

	Set ML = Doc.RunMacro3("MAKR7S.BAGLANTI", "GetBaglantiliMPSList", EvrakTuru, EvrakNo, TRNum)
	Set M10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set M10T = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
	Set M10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	For i = 1 to ML.GetRowCount()
		Doc.Load_Voucher ML.EVRAKNO(i)
		if M10TM.GetRowCount() = 1 and Doc.Round(M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, 2) = Doc.Round(ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i), 2) Then
			M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE = ""
			M10TM.KAYNAKMPS = ""
			M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM = ""
			M10E.ACIK_KAPALI = "K"
		Else
			For j = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
				if M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(j) = ML.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(i) and M10TM.KAYNAKMPS(j) = ML.KAYNAKMPS(i) and _
																											M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(j) = ML.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(i) Then
					M10TM.SetCurrentRow(j)
					EskiToplamMiktar = M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR
					EskiM10EToplamMiktar = M10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR
					if Doc.Round(M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, 2) = Doc.Round(ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i), 2) Then
						M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU = ""
						M10TM.SF_PAKETSAYISI = 0
						M10TM.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0
						M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE = ""
						M10TM.KAYNAKMPS = ""
						M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM = ""
						M10TM.MUSTERIKODU = ""
						M10E.ACIK_KAPALI = ""
					Else
						M10TM.SF_PAKETICERIGI = Doc.Div(M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR - ML.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(i), M10TM.SF_PAKETSAYISI)
						YeniToplamMiktar = M10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR
					End If
					M10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI  = Doc.Div(M10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR - (EskiToplamMiktar - YeniToplamMiktar), M10E.SF_PAKETSAYISI)
					Oran = Doc.Div(M10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, EskiM10EToplamMiktar)
					Exit For
				End if
			Next
			'msgbox oran
			For j = 1 to MMPS10T.GetRowCount()
				M10T.SetCurrentRow(j)
				M10T.R_MIKTAR0 = M10T.R_MIKTAR0 * Oran
				M10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI = M10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI * Oran
				M10T.R_MIKTART_ALT = M10T.R_MIKTART_ALT * Oran
			Next
		End if
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	Next
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MPSHESAPLA
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : MPS 'i Tekrar Hesapla
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	MMPS10TM.SafePack()
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select2("drtt677", "MMPS10TM", "", "", -1)
	MMPS10E.FIXEDRECETE = "H"
	Doc.RunCommand "UID_RECALC_MPS"
	MMPS10E.FIXEDRECETE = "E"
	For i = 1 to MMPS10TM.GetRowCount()
		MMPS10TM.SetCurrentRow(i)
		if trim(M10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO) <> "" Then 
			PaketIcerigi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T", "SF_IMIKTAR", MMPS10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO, "ARTNO")
			if PaketIcerigi = 0 Then PaketIcerigi = MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR
			PaketSayisi = Doc.Div(MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, PaketIcerigi)
			if PaketSayisi <> Doc.Round(PaketSayisi, 0) Then
				PaketSayisi = 1
				PaketIcerigi = MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR
			End if
			MMPS10TM.SF_PAKETSAYISI = PaketSayisi
			MMPS10TM.SF_PAKETICERIGI = PaketIcerigi
		End if
		MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE = M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE
		MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS = M10TM.KAYNAKMPS
		MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM = M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM
	Next
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MPSKAPAT
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Kaynak MPS 'i kapalý olan MPS 'leri kapat
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set g = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Set M10E = Doc.Select1("sfdf3fff", "MMPS10E", "ACIK_KAPALI=' '", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to M10E.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS 'ler Kontrol Ediliyor : " & i & " / " & M10E.GetRowCount()
		if KaynakIsVeyaMPSKapaliMi(M10E.EVRAKNO(i)) Then
			Doc.Load_Voucher M10E.EVRAKNO(i)
			MMPS10E.ACIK_KAPALI = "K"
			Doc.Save_Voucher
			Doc.d7MsgBox M10E.EVRAKNO(i) & " no.lu MPS kapatýldý", "", MB_OK
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function KaynakIsVeyaMPSKapaliMi(MPSNo)
	KaynakIsVeyaMPSKapaliMi = False
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("dwef3ff", "MMPS10TM", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE<>' '", "", -1)
	if M10TM.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	KaynakIsVeyaMPSKapaliMi = True
	For i = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		if not KaynakKapaliMi(M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(i), M10TM.KAYNAKMPS(i), M10TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(i)) Then
			KaynakIsVeyaMPSKapaliMi = False
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function KaynakKapaliMi(KaynakEvrakType, MPSNo, TRNum)
	KaynakKapaliMi = False
	if KaynakEvrakType = "SP" Then
		AK = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T", "ACIKKAPALI", MPSNo & TRNum, "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
		if trim(AK) = "K" Then KaynakKapaliMi = True
	Elseif KaynakEvrakType = "UP" Then
		KaynakKapaliMi = KaynakMPSKapaliMi(MPSNo, TRNum)
	End if
End Function
Function KaynakMPSKapaliMi(MPSNo, TRNum)
	KaynakMPSKapaliMi = False
	SiraNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_SIRANO", MPSNo & TRNum, "EVRAKNO+TRNUM")
	Set M10TI = Doc.Select1("afe3f", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&R_SIRANO='" & siraNo & "'", "", -1)
	if M10TI.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	if trim(M10TI.R_ACIK_KAPALI(1)) = "K" or trim(M10TI.ACIK_KAPALI(1)) = "K" Then
		KaynakMPSKapaliMi = True
	End if
	'MSGBOX MPSNo & ", " & KaynakKapaliMi
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Makro Kodu : MPSSEC
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS301
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Bir Sipariþe ait MPS 'lerin Seçimi
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "SIPNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "B_REV_RTESTARIH", "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "E_REV_RTESTARIH", "DATE",10,0,"L","",""

	Set MTableT1=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET1=STACK","IO")
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "TRNUM", "CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SRNUM", "CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "ARTNO", "CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "MUSTERIKODU", "CHAR",16,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('CARI00')",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_SF_UNIT", "CHAR",6,0,"L","","STUNIT"
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "REV_RTESTARIH", "DATE",10,0,"D","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	Set MTableT2 = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET2=MMPS10TM(NOFILE)","IO")

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub SiprisListesiniGetir()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	MTableT1.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	MTableT1.Empty
	MTableT2.Empty

	QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & MTable.SIPNO & "'&REV_RTESTARIH>='" & MTable.B_REV_RTESTARIH & "'"
	if trim(MTable.B_REV_RTESTARIH) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & "&REV_RTESTARIH<='" & MTable.E_REV_RTESTARIH & "'"
	End if
	Set S40T = Doc.Select1("dde34r55", "STOK40T", QStr, "SRNUM", -1)
	if S40T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	For i = 1 to S40T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Sipariþler Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S40T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT1.AddRow()
		MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT1.TRNUM = S40T.TRNUM(i)
		MTableT1.SRNUM = S40T.SRNUM(i)
		MTableT1.KOD = S40T.KOD(i)
		MTableT1.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT1.KOD, "")
		MTableT1.SF_MIKTAR = S40T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT1.SF_SF_UNIT = S40T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		MTableT1.MUSTERIKODU = S40T.MUSTERIKODU(i)
		MTableT1.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", S40T.MUSTERIKODU(i), "")
		MTableT1.EVRAKNO = S40T.EVRAKNO(i)
		MTableT1.ARTNO = S40T.ARTNO(i)
		MTableT1.REV_RTESTARIH = S40T.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
		MTableT1.SELECTED = "E"
	Next
	MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT1.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub MPSleriGetir()
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	Set M30T = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS30T")
	M30T.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	M30T.Empty
	Set s = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	For i = 1 to MTableT1.GetRowCount()
		if MTableT1.SELECTED(i) = "E" Then
			Call SecilenSipariseAitMPSleriGetir(i)
		End if
	Next
	M30T.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
Sub SecilenSipariseAitMPSleriGetir(sNo)
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	Set M30T = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS30T")

	ET = "SP"
	ENo = MTableT1.EVRAKNO(sNo)
	TRNum = MTableT1.TRNUM(sNo)
	Set MPSList = Doc.RunMacro3("MAKR7S.BAGLANTI", "GetBaglantiliMPSList", ET, ENo, TRNum)
	if MPSList.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTableT1.EVRAKNO(sNo)) & " no.lu sipariþin " & MTableT1.SRNUM(sNo) & _
						" no.lu satýrýna ait MPS bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
	End if
	For i = 1 to MPSList.GetRowCount()
		Set M30T1 = Doc.Select2("weffs44g", "MMPS30T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSList.EVRAKNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if M30T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS ekleniyor : " & MPSList.EVRAKNO(i)
			satirNo = M30T.AddRow
			M30T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			M30T.EVRAKNO = MPSList.EVRAKNO(i)
			M30T.MAMULSTOKKODU = MPSList.MAMULSTOKKODU(i)
			M30T.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M30T.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
			Set M10E = Doc.Select1("sgsgsg", "MMPS10E", "EVRAKNO='" & M30T.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
			M30T.STATUS = M10E.STATUS
			if trim(M30T.STATUS) <> "R" Then
				M30T.SELECTED = "E"
			Else
				M30T.SELECTED = " "
			End if
			M30T.SF_PAKETSAYISI = M10E.SF_PAKETSAYISI(1)
			M30T.SF_PAKETICERIGI = M10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI(1)
			M30T.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR = M10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)
			M30T.MUSTERIKODU = M10E.MUSTERIKODU
			M30T.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", M30T.MUSTERIKODU, "")
			M30T.MUSTERISIPINO = M10E.MUSTERISIPINO
			M30T.MUSTERISIPIARTNO = M10E.MUSTERISIPIARTNO
			M30T.URETIMSIPINO = M10E.URETIMSIPINO
			M30T.URETIMSIPIARTNO = M10E.URETIMSIPIARTNO
			M30T.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH = M10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH
			M30T.URETIMDENTESTARIH = M10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH
			M30T.SF_SF_UNIT = M10E.SF_SF_UNIT
			M30T.TOLERANSTYPE = M10E.TOLERANSTYPE
			M30T.TOLERANS = M10E.TOLERANS
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Sub SecilenMPSeAitAltMPSleriGetir()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set M30T = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS30T")
	M30T.SafePack()

	ET = "UP"
	ENo = MTable.EVRAKNO
	TRNum = ""
	Set MPSList = Doc.RunMacro3("MAKR7S.BAGLANTI", "GetBaglantiliMPSList1", ET, ENo, TRNum)
	if MPSList.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MTable.EVRAKNO) & " no.lu MPS bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
	End if
	M30T.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	For i = 1 to MPSList.GetRowCount()
		Set M30T1 = Doc.Select2("weffs44g", "MMPS30T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSList.EVRAKNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if M30T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS ekleniyor : " & MPSList.EVRAKNO(i)
			satirNo = M30T.AddRow
			M30T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			M30T.EVRAKNO = MPSList.EVRAKNO(i)
			M30T.MAMULSTOKKODU = MPSList.MAMULSTOKKODU(i)
			M30T.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M30T.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
			Set M10E = Doc.Select1("sgsgsg", "MMPS10E", "EVRAKNO='" & M30T.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
			M30T.STATUS = M10E.STATUS
			if trim(M30T.STATUS) <> "R" Then
				M30T.SELECTED = "E"
			Else
				M30T.SELECTED = " "
			End if
			M30T.SF_PAKETSAYISI = M10E.SF_PAKETSAYISI(1)
			M30T.SF_PAKETICERIGI = M10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI(1)
			M30T.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR = M10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)
			M30T.MUSTERIKODU = M10E.MUSTERIKODU
			M30T.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", M30T.MUSTERIKODU, "")
			M30T.MUSTERISIPINO = M10E.MUSTERISIPINO
			M30T.MUSTERISIPIARTNO = M10E.MUSTERISIPIARTNO
			M30T.URETIMSIPINO = M10E.URETIMSIPINO
			M30T.URETIMSIPIARTNO = M10E.URETIMSIPIARTNO
			M30T.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH = M10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH
			M30T.URETIMDENTESTARIH = M10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH
			M30T.SF_SF_UNIT = M10E.SF_SF_UNIT
			M30T.TOLERANSTYPE = M10E.TOLERANSTYPE
			M30T.TOLERANS = M10E.TOLERANS
		End if
	Next
	M30T.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MUH
Hangi Tablo için : TECH10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Excel 'den Mühendislik Tanýmlarýný Aktar
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set SSS = doc.CreateSobject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\muhendislik2.xls", False, True)
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	For i = 5 to 65000
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Mühendislik Tanýmlarý Aktarýlýyor : " & i
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value) = "" Then Exit For
		FoyNo = MuhFoyNo(Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value, Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value)
		YeniSatir = True
		For j = 6 to 255
			TSKodu = Sh1.Cells(3,j).Value
			if trim(TSKodu) = "" Then Exit For
			MuhendislikTanimi = Sh1.Cells(i,j).Value
			if trim(MuhendislikTanimi) <> "" Then
				if YeniSatir Then
					if trim(TECH10E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
					if trim(FoyNo) <> "" Then 
						Doc.Load_Voucher FoyNo
						TECH10T.Empty
					Else
						Doc.New_Voucher()
						TECH10E.KRITERCODE_1 = "SKOD"
						TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_1 = Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
						TECH10E.KRITERCODE_2 = "OKOD"
						TECH10E.KRITERVALUE_2 = Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value
					End if
				End if
				YeniSatir = False
				MuhArr = GetYaziArr(CStr(MuhendislikTanimi), 70)
				For Each MuhTanim In MuhArr
					satirNo = TECH10T.AddRow
					TECH10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
					TECH10T.TSCODE(satirNo) = TSKodu
					TECH10T.TSTEXT(satirNo) = MuhTanim
				Next
			End If
		Next
	Next
	if trim(TECH10E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
End Sub
Function MuhFoyNo(StokKodu, Operasyonkodu)
	MuhFoyNo = ""

	QStr = "KRITERVALUE_1='" & StokKodu & "'&KRITERVALUE_2='" & OperasyonKodu & "'"
	Set M11 = Doc.Select1("sdsgfs", "TECH10E", QStr, "", -1)
	if M11.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		MuhFoyNo = M11.EVRAKNO
	End if
End Function
Function GetYaziArr(Yazi, StrLen)
	ReDim StrArr(0)
	Yazi = trim(Yazi)
	if Len(Yazi) <= StrLen Then
		StrArr(0) = Yazi
	else
		Do
			if Len(Yazi) <= StrLen Then
				StrArr(uBound(StrArr)) = Yazi
				Exit Do
			Else
				Bosluk = 0
				For i = (StrLen + 1) To 1 Step -1
					if Mid(Yazi, i, 1) = " " Then
						Bosluk = i
						Exit For
					End if
				Next
				if Bosluk = 0 then Bosluk = StrLen + 1
				StrArr(uBound(StrArr)) = Left(Yazi, Bosluk-1)
				Yazi = trim(Mid(Yazi, Bosluk))
				Redim Preserve StrArr(uBound(StrArr) + 1)
			End if
		Loop
	End if
	GetYaziArr = StrArr
End Function

Hangi Tablo için : BOMU01
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Üzerinde Bulunulan Satýrýn Mühendislik Tanýmýný Göster/Yeni Yarat
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	if BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE <> "I" Then 
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Operasyon satýrýnýn üzerinde bulunmalýsýnýz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	QStr = "KRITERCODE_1='SKOD'&KRITERVALUE_1='" & BOMU01E.MAMULCODE &"'"
	QStr = QStr & "&KRITERCODE_2='OKOD'&KRITERVALUE_2='" & BOMU01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON &"'"
	Set T10 = Doc.Select1("dde4344r5", "TECH10E", QStr, "", -1)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("TECH10", "")
	Set T10E = Doc1.GetTableObject("TECH10E")
	Set T10T = Doc1.GetTableObject("TECH10T")

	if T10.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc1.New_Voucher()
		T10E.KRITERCODE_1 = "SKOD"	
		T10E.KRITERVALUE_1 = BOMU01E.MAMULCODE
		T10E.KRITERCODE_2 = "OKOD"	
		T10E.KRITERVALUE_2 = BOMU01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON
	Elseif T10.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		Doc1.Load_Voucher T10.EVRAKNO
	Else
		Str1 = "Bu ürün ve operasyona ait birden çok mühendislik tanýmý bulundu." & vcrlf & _
					"Ekrana getirmek istediðiniz mühendislik tanýmýný seçiniz" & vbcrlf
		For i = 1 to T10.GetRowCount()
			Str2 = i & ". Föy No : " & trim(T10.EVRAKNO(i)) 
			if (T10.KRITERCODE_3(i)) = "CKOD" Then
				Str2 = Str2 & ", Müþteri : " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(T10.KRITERVALUE_3(i)), 16), "")
			End if
			Str1 = Str1 & Str2 & vbcrlf
		Next
		k = InputBox(Str1, "freedom", 1)
		if CInt(k) > 0 and CInt(k) <= T10.GetRowCount() Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher T10.EVRAKNO(cInt(k))
		End if
	End if
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MUH-2
Hangi Tablo için : TECH10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Bir Koda Ait Mühendislik Tanýmlarýný Diðerine Kopyala
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set MTable = Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD1","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD2","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""

	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub Kopyala()
	Set MTable=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	OncekiKod = MTable.KOD1
	YeniKod = MTable.KOD2
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL

	Set T10E = Doc.GetTableObject("TECH10E")
	QStr = "KRITERCODE_1='SKOD'&KRITERVALUE_1='" & OncekiKod &"'"
	Set T10 = Doc.Select1("dde4344r5", "TECH10E", QStr, "", -1)
	For i = 1 to T10.GetRowCount()
		Doc.Load_Voucher T10.EVRAKNO(i)
		Doc.RunCommand "MT_DOC_COPY"
		FoyNo = MuhFoyNo(YeniKod, T10E.KRITERVALUE_2)
		if trim(FoyNo) = "" Then
			Doc.New_Voucher()
		Else
			Doc.Load_Voucher FoyNo
		End if
		Doc.RunCommand "MT_DOC_PASTE"
		T10E.KRITERVALUE_1 = YeniKod
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	Next
End Sub
Function MuhFoyNo(StokKodu, Operasyonkodu)
	MuhFoyNo = ""

	QStr = "KRITERVALUE_1='" & StokKodu & "'&KRITERVALUE_2='" & OperasyonKodu & "'"
	Set M11 = Doc.Select1("sdsgfs", "TECH10E", QStr, "", -1)
	if M11.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		MuhFoyNo = M11.EVRAKNO
	End if
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	if MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE <> "I" Then 
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Operasyon satýrýnýn üzerinde bulunmalýsýnýz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	QStr = "KRITERCODE_1='SKOD'&KRITERVALUE_1='" & MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU &"'"
	QStr = QStr & "&KRITERCODE_2='OKOD'&KRITERVALUE_2='" & MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON &"'"
	Set T10 = Doc.Select1("dde4344r5", "TECH10E", QStr, "", -1)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("TECH10", "")
	Set T10E = Doc1.GetTableObject("TECH10E")
	Set T10T = Doc1.GetTableObject("TECH10T")

	if T10.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc1.New_Voucher()
		T10E.KRITERCODE_1 = "SKOD"	
		T10E.KRITERVALUE_1 = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU
		T10E.KRITERCODE_2 = "OKOD"	
		T10E.KRITERVALUE_2 = MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON
	Elseif T10.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		Doc1.Load_Voucher T10.EVRAKNO
	Else
		Str1 = "Bu ürün ve operasyona ait birden çok mühendislik tanýmý bulundu." & vcrlf & _
					"Ekrana getirmek istediðiniz mühendislik tanýmýný seçiniz" & vbcrlf
		For i = 1 to T10.GetRowCount()
			Str2 = i & ". Föy No : " & trim(T10.EVRAKNO(i)) 
			if (T10.KRITERCODE_3(i)) = "CKOD" Then
				Str2 = Str2 & ", Müþteri : " & Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(T10.KRITERVALUE_3(i)), 16), "")
			End if
			Str1 = Str1 & Str2 & vbcrlf
		Next
		k = InputBox(Str1, "freedom", 1)
		if CInt(k) > 0 and CInt(k) <= T10.GetRowCount() Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher T10.EVRAKNO(cInt(k))
		End if
	End if
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : OSF312
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set SFDC31E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC31E")
	SFDC31E.Setd7FieldEvent "JOBNO","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "JOBNO_AV"
	SFDC31E.Setd7FieldEvent "MPSNO","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "MPSNO_AV"
	SFDC31E.Setd7FieldEvent "RECTARIH1","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "RECTARIH1_AV"
	SFDC31E.Setd7FieldEvent "X_T_ISMERKEZI","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "X_T_ISMERKEZI_AV"
End Sub
Sub UretimFisiOlustur()
	Set SFDC31E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC31E")
	if trim(SFDC31E.GK_1) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "'Makara Bitti mi?' alaný doldurulmamýþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if trim(SFDC31E.GK_1) <> "E" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bitmemiþ makaralar için üretim fiþi oluþturulamaz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_URETIM_FISI"	
	Set STOK20_OLUSTURMA_KRITERLERI = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK20_OLUSTURMA_KRITERLERI")
	STOK20_OLUSTURMA_KRITERLERI.AMBCODE = "8"
	STOK20_OLUSTURMA_KRITERLERI.IMALATAMBCODE = "8"
	STOK20_OLUSTURMA_KRITERLERI.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "8"

	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_URETIM_FISI2"
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
Sub X_T_ISMERKEZI_AV()
	Call OtomatikLot()
End Sub
Sub RECTARIH1_AV()
	Call OtomatikLot()
End Sub
Sub JOBNO_AV()
	ret = MamulseUretimFisiButonuPasif()
	if ret = 0 Then
		Call YariMamulseUretimFisiButonuPasif()
	End if
	Call OtomatikLot()
	Call GetHmdList()
End Sub
Sub MPSNO_AV()
	ret = MamulseUretimFisiButonuPasif()
	Call TekIsVarsaGetir()
End Sub
Function MamulseUretimFisiButonuPasif()
	MamulseUretimFisiButonuPasif = 0
	Set SFDC31E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC31E")
	ButonId = "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME & "','UretimFisiOlustur')"
	if Left(SFDC31E.STOK_CODE, 1) = "3" Then
		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "", ButonId, "READONLY", 1
		MamulseUretimFisiButonuPasif = 1
	Else
		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "", ButonId, "READONLY", 0
	End if
End Function
Sub YariMamulseUretimFisiButonuPasif()
	Set SFDC31E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC31E")
	ButonId = "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME & "','UretimFisiOlustur')"

	SrNum1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", SFDC31E.JOBNO, "JOBNO")
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("adf3fafgda", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & SFDC31E.MPSNO & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM>'" & SrNum1 & "'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "", ButonId, "READONLY", 1
	Else
		Doc.SetFormControlProperty "", ButonId, "READONLY", 0
	End if
End Sub
Sub UretimFisiSil()
	Set SFDC31E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC31E")
	Set S20T = Doc.Select1("aef3fsds4sh", "STOK20T", "W_APP='OSF312'&W_TABLE='SFDC31E'&W_EVRAKNO='" & SFDC31E.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
	if S20T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Üretim Fiþi Oluþturulmamýþ", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher S20T.EVRAKNO
	Doc1.Delete_Voucher
End Sub
Sub TekIsVarsaGetir()
	Set SFDC31E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC31E")
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("adf23gaeg", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & SFDC31E.MPSNO & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() <> 1 Then Exit Sub

	SFDC31E.JOBNO = M10T.JOBNO
	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "SFDC31E","JOBNO",1
	Call JOBNO_AV()
End Sub
Sub OtomatikLot()
	Set SFDC31E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC31E")
	LotGirilmis = False
	if trim(SFDC31E.LOTNUMBER) <> trim(SFDC31E.EVRAKNO) Then
		LotNo = Trim(SFDC31E.LOTNUMBER)
		if Len(LotNo) > 1 Then
			if Right(LotNo, 1) <> "-" Then LotGirilmis = True
		End if
	End if
	if LotGirilmis Then Exit Sub

	if trim(SFDC31E.X_T_ISMERKEZI) = "" or trim(SFDC31E.RECTARIH1) = "" Then Exit Sub

	SFDC31E.LOTNUMBER = trim(SFDC31E.X_T_ISMERKEZI) & "/" & Mid(SFDC31E.RECTARIH1, 3,2) & "-"
End Sub
Sub GetHmdList()
	Set S31E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC31E")
	Set S31T = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC31T")

	if trim(S31E.JOBNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	S31T.Empty
	SiraNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_SIRANO", S31E.JOBNO, "JOBNO")
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("dededfff2", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & S31E.MPSNO &"'&R_SIRANO='" & SiraNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", -1)
	OncekiOpJobNo = GetOncekiOpJobNo(S31E.MPSNO, S31E.JOBNO)
	if trim(OncekiOpJobNo) <> "" Then
		OncekiOp = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_OPERASYON", OncekiOpJobNo, "JOBNO")
		if trim(OncekiOp) = "AKTY" and trim(S31E.OPERASYON) = "DB" Then
			OncekiOpSiraNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_SIRANO", OncekiJobNo, "JOBNO")
			QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & S31E.MPSNO &"'&R_SIRANO='" & OncekiOpSiraNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'"
			Set M10TO = Doc.Select1("dededfcceff2", "MMPS10T", QStr, "", -1)
			For i = 1 to M10TO.GetRowCount()
				satirNo = S31T.AddRow
				S31T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				S31T.KOD(satirNo) = M10TO.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
				S31T.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S31T.KOD(satirNo), "")
				S31T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", S31T.KOD(satirNo), "")
			Next
		Else
			satirNo = S31T.AddRow
			S31T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S31T.KOD(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "MAMULSTOKKODU", S31E.MPSNO, "")
			S31T.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S31T.KOD(satirNo), "")
			S31T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", S31T.KOD(satirNo), "")
		End if
	End if
	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S31T.AddRow
		S31T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S31T.KOD(satirNo) = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
		S31T.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S31T.KOD(satirNo), "")
		S31T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", S31T.KOD(satirNo), "")
	Next
	if S31T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then S31T.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub
Function GetOncekiOpJobNo(MPSNo, IsNo)
	GetOncekiOpJobNo = ""
	Sirano = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", IsNo, "JOBNO")
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("ded3fff4f", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&SRNUM<'" & SiraNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "<SRNUM", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetOncekiOpJobNo = M10T.JOBNO(1)
End Function
Sub IsBrowse()
	Set BRW_MMPS10T = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("USR_BRW_MMPS10T=MMPS10T(NOFILE)", "IO")
	BRW_MMPS10T.Addfield2 "R_BAKIYESURE","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "USR_BRW_MMPS10T_JOBNO",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
	Call IsBilgileriniDoldur()
End Sub
Sub IsBilgileriniDoldur()
	Set SFDC31E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC31E")
	Set BRW_MMPS10T = Doc.GetTableObject("USR_BRW_MMPS10T")
	if trim(SFDC31E.MPSNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("fsdgfs4s", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & SFDC31E.MPSNO & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "", -1)
	BRW_MMPS10T.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	BRW_MMPS10T.Empty
	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = BRW_MMPS10T.AddRow
		BRW_MMPS10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		BRW_MMPS10T.EVRAKNO = M10T.EVRAKNO(i)
		BRW_MMPS10T.JOBNO = M10T.JOBNO(i)
		BRW_MMPS10T.R_ACIK_KAPALI = M10T.R_ACIK_KAPALI(i)
		BRW_MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE = M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i)
		BRW_MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
		BRW_MMPS10T.KAYNAK_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), "")
		BRW_MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON = M10T.R_OPERASYON(i)
		BRW_MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON_IMLT01_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", M10T.R_OPERASYON(i), "")
		BRW_MMPS10T.R_MIKTART = M10T.R_MIKTART(i)
		BRW_MMPS10T.R_YMAMULMIKTAR = M10T.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(i)
		BRW_MMPS10T.R_TMYMAMULMIKTAR = M10T.R_TMYMAMULMIKTAR(i)
		BRW_MMPS10T.R_BAKIYEYMAMULMIKTAR = M10T.R_BAKIYEYMAMULMIKTAR(i)
		if M10T.R_BAKIYEYMAMULMIKTAR(i) > 0 AND M10T.R_TMYMAMULMIKTAR(i) = 0 Then
			BRW_MMPS10T.R_BAKIYESURE = M10T.R_MIKTART(i)
		Else
			BRW_MMPS10T.R_BAKIYESURE = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(M10T.R_MIKTAR0(i) * M10T.R_BAKIYEYMAMULMIKTAR(i), M10T.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(i)), 2)
		End if
	Next
	if BRW_MMPS10T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then BRW_MMPS10T.SetCurrentRow(1)
	BRW_MMPS10T.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub IsSec()
	Set SFDC31E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC31E")
	Set BRW_MMPS10T = Doc.GetTableObject("USR_BRW_MMPS10T")

	SFDC31E.JOBNO = BRW_MMPS10T.JOBNO
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : REZ
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Tüm Sipariþlere Ait Rezerveleri Getir (Ýþ Emri Planlama Paneli Ýçin)
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set f = doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Doc.CreateSObject("Parameters")

	For i = 1 to M22_MMPS10E_LIST.GetRowCount()

		Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_NAME_001",M22_MMPS10E_LIST.GetAppName()	
		Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_CURENT_ROW",i
		Doc.RunCommand1 "CMD_SET_CURRENT_ROW",Par

		For j = 1 to M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.GetRowCount()
			Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_NAME_001",M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.GetAppName()	
			Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_CURENT_ROW",j
			Doc.RunCommand1 "CMD_SET_CURRENT_ROW",Par

			Set S01G = Doc.Select1G("ddfr5gt667", "STOK01", "ALLOC_SIPARTNO='" & M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.STOK40T_ARTNO & "'", "", "ALLOC_SIPARTNO", -1)
			if S01G.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_TRANSFER_DIKKATE_ALINSIN = "1"
				M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_HM_NET_TRANSFER = S01G.SF_MIKTAR
				Bakiye = M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_HM_BAKIYE - S01G.SF_MIKTAR
				if Bakiye < 0 Then Bakiye = 0
				M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_HM_BAKIYE = Bakiye
			End if

			Set S40T2G = Doc.Select1G("ddfr5gt667", "STOK40T2", "ARTNO='" & M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.STOK40T_ARTNO & "'", "", "ARTNO", -1)
			if S40T2G.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_TRANSFER_DIKKATE_ALINSIN = "1"
				M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_HM_NET_TRANSFER = M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_HM_NET_TRANSFER + S40T2G.SF_MIKTAR
				Bakiye = M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_HM_BAKIYE - S40T2G.SF_MIKTAR
				if Bakiye < 0 Then Bakiye = 0
				M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_HM_BAKIYE = Bakiye
			End if

			if M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_HM_BAKIYE = 0 Then M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.M22_SELECTED = "0"
		Next
	Next
	Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_NAME_001",M22_MMPS10E_LIST.GetAppName()	
	Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_CURENT_ROW",1
	Doc.RunCommand1 "CMD_SET_CURRENT_ROW",Par
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : REZERVE
Hangi Tablo için : STOK32
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Stoktan Rezervasyon
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "B_KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "E_KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE", "CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "REZ_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
	MTable.B_KOD = "3"
	MTable.E_KOD = "3zzz"
	MTable.AMBCODE = "3"
End Sub
Sub STOK01_Doldur()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if trim(MTable.AMBCODE) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Depo Boþ Olamaz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	end if

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	QStr = "KOD>='" & MTable.B_KOD & "'&ALLOC_SYSSTATUS=' '"
	if trim(MTable.E_KOD) <> "" Then 
		QStr = QStr & "&KOD<='" & MTable.E_KOD & "'"
	End if
	if trim(MTable.AMBCODE) <> "" Then 
		QStr = QStr & "&AMBCODE='" & MTable.AMBCODE & "'"
	End if
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1G("dde34r55", "STOK01", QStr, "KOD", "KOD", -1)
	MTableT.Empty()
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		RezerveEdilebilecekMiktar = GetRezerveEdilebilecekMiktar(S01.KOD(i))
		if RezerveEdilebilecekMiktar > 0 Then		
			satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
			MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			MTableT.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
			MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD, "")
			MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			MTableT.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
			MTableT.REZ_MIKTAR = RezerveEdilebilecekMiktar
		End if
	Next
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub RezervasyonListesi()
	Dim MTableT1
	Dim MTableT2
	Set MTableT1=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET1=STACK","IO")
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "ARTNO", "CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "MUSTERIKODU", "CHAR",16,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('CARI00')",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_SF_UNIT", "CHAR",6,0,"L","","STUNIT"
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "REZ_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	Set MTableT2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET2=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "REZ_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT2.Setd7FieldEvent "SELECTED","ONVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SELECTED_OV"
	MTableT2.Setd7FieldEvent "REZ_MIKTAR","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "REZ_MIKTAR_AV"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
	Call SiparisveStokDetaylariniGetir()
End Sub
Sub SiparisveStokDetaylariniGetir()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	StokKodu = MTableT.KOD
	if trim(StokKodu) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Call SiprisListesiniGetir()
	Call DetayStokListesiniGetir()
End Sub
Sub SiprisListesiniGetir()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	MTableT1.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	QStr = "ACIKKAPALI=' '&KOD='" & MTableT.KOD & "'"
	Set S40T = Doc.Select1("dde34r55", "STOK40T", QStr, "KOD", -1)
	MTableT1.Empty()
	if S40T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	For i = 1 to S40T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Sipariþler Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S40T.GetRowCount()
		SipeRezerveStok = GetSipeRezerveStok(S40T.KOD(i), S40T.ARTNO(i))
		RezerveEdilebilecekMiktar = S40T.SF_MIKTAR(i) - S40T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(i) - SipeRezerveStok
		if RezerveEdilebilecekMiktar > 0 Then
	
			satirNo = MTableT1.AddRow()
			MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			MTableT1.KOD = S40T.KOD(i)
			MTableT1.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT1.KOD, "")
			MTableT1.SF_MIKTAR = S40T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			MTableT1.SF_SF_UNIT = S40T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
			MTableT1.REZ_MIKTAR = RezerveEdilebilecekMiktar
			MTableT1.MUSTERIKODU = S40T.MUSTERIKODU(i)
			MTableT1.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", S40T.MUSTERIKODU(i), "")
			MTableT1.EVRAKNO = S40T.EVRAKNO(i)
			MTableT1.ARTNO = S40T.ARTNO(i)
		End if
	Next
	MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT1.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub DetayStokListesiniGetir()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	QStr = "KOD='" & MTableT.KOD & "'&ALLOC_SYSSTATUS=' '"
	if trim(MTable.AMBCODE) <> "" Then 
		QStr = QStr & "&AMBCODE='" & MTable.AMBCODE & "'"
	End if
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("dde34r55", "STOK01", QStr, "KOD", -1)
	MTableT2.Empty()
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Stok Listesi Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow()
		MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT2.SELECTED = "H"
		MTableT2.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
		MTableT2.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT2.KOD, "")
		MTableT2.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		MTableT2.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		MTableT2.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT2.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT2.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
	Next
	MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = 0
End Sub
Sub RezervasyonlariFiseAktar()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set S32E = Doc.GetTableobject("STOK32E")
	Set S32T = Doc.GetTableobject("STOK32T")
	Set MT1 = Doc.Select2("dr44t67", "MAKROTABLET1", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Sipariþ Seçilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if MT1.GetRowCount() > 1 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Yalnýzca 1 sipariþ satýrý iþaretlenebilir.", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set MT2 = Doc.Select2("dr44t672", "MAKROTABLET2", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Rezervasyon Yapýlacak Stok Seçilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	S32T.Empty
	SipArtNo = MT1.ARTNO
	For i = 1 to MT2.GetRowCount()
		if i = 1 Then
			Doc.New_Voucher()
			S32E.AMBCODE = MTable.AMBCODE
		End if
		satirNo = S32T.AddRow()
		S32T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S32T.KOD = MT2.KOD(i)
		S32T.SF_MIKTAR = MT2.REZ_MIKTAR(i)
		S32T.SF_SF_UNIT = MT2.SF_UNIT(i)
		S32T.LOTNUMBER = MT2.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S32T.SERINO = MT2.SERINO(i)
		S32T.FOKOD = MT2.FOKOD(i)
		S32T.FARKLIDAGITIM = "*"
		S32T.REAL_SIPARTNO = SipArtNo
		S32T.SF_VRI_VR_C = MT2.VRI_VR_C(i)
		S32T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MT2.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S32T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MT2.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S32T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MT2.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		S32T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MT2.VRI_NUM1(i)
		S32T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MT2.VRI_NUM2(i)
		S32T.LOCATION1 = MT2.LOCATION1(i)
		S32T.LOCATION2 = MT2.LOCATION2(i)
		S32T.LOCATION3 = MT2.LOCATION3(i)
		S32T.LOCATION4 = MT2.LOCATION4(i)
	Next
	Doc.Save_Voucher()
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
Sub SELECTED_OV()
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if MTableT2.SELECTED = "E" Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC + MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR
	Else
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC - MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR
	End if
End Sub
Sub REZ_MIKTAR_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MT2 = Doc.Select2("ee4r56", "MAKROTABLET2", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = 0
		Exit Sub
	End if
	MT2.GroupBy("KOD")
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MT2.REZ_MIKTAR
End Sub
Function S60TdeYok(S01, i)
	S60TdeYok = True
	QStr = "KOD='" & S01.KOD(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&LOTNUMBER='" & S01.LOTNUMBER(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&SERINO='" & S01.SERINO(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&FOKOD='" & S01.FOKOD(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&LOCATION1='" & S01.LOCATION1(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&LOCATION2='" & S01.LOCATION2(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&LOCATION3='" & S01.LOCATION3(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&LOCATION4='" & S01.LOCATION4(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&SF_SF_UNIT='" & S01.SF_UNIT(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&SF_VRI_VR_C='" & S01.VRI_VR_C(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&SF_VRI_VR_R1='" & S01.VRI_VR_R1(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&SF_VRI_VR_R2='" & S01.VRI_VR_R2(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&SF_VRI_VR_R3='" & S01.VRI_VR_R3(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&SF_VRI_NUM1='" & S01.VRI_NUM1(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&SF_VRI_NUM2='" & S01.VRI_NUM2(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&ALLOC_SYSSTATUS='" & S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&ALLOC_USTATUS='" & S01.ALLOC_USTATUS(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&ALLOC_NONMOVABL='" & S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&ALLOC_SIPTYPE='" & S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE='" & S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&ALLOC_EVRAKNO='" & S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&ALLOC_SIPNO='" & S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&ALLOC_SIPARTNO='" & S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&ALLOC_MPSEVNO='" & S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&ALLOC_MPSARTNO='" & S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i) & "'"

	Set S60T = Doc.Select2("dff55__dd4", "STOK60T", QStr, "", -1)
	if S60T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then S60TdeYok = False
End Function
Function GetRezerveEdilebilecekMiktar(StokKodu)
	GetRezerveEdilebilecekMiktar = 0
	Set S40T = Doc.Select1("d35t54t64y", "STOK40T", "ACIKKAPALI=' '&KOD='" & StokKodu & "'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to S40T.GetRowCount()
		SipeRezerveStok = 0
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1G("dr5yr78u7", "STOK01", "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'&ALLOC_SIPARTNO='" & S40T.ARTNO(i) & "'&ALLOC_SYSSTATUS='2'", "", "KOD", -1)
		if S01.GetRowCount() > 0 Then SipeRezerveStok = S01.SF_MIKTAR
		RezerveEdilebilecekMiktar = S40T.SF_MIKTAR(i) - S40T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(i) - SipeRezerveStok
		if RezerveEdilebilecekMiktar > 0 Then
			GetRezerveEdilebilecekMiktar = GetRezerveEdilebilecekMiktar + RezerveEdilebilecekMiktar
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function GetSipeRezerveStok(StokKodu,ArtNo)
	GetSipeRezerveStok = 0
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1G("dr5yr78u7", "STOK01", "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'&ALLOC_SIPARTNO='" & ArtNo & "'&ALLOC_SYSSTATUS='2'", "", "KOD", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() > 0 Then GetSipeRezerveStok = S01.SF_MIKTAR
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : REZERVE2
Hangi Tablo için : STOK40
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Stoktan Rezervasyon
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "AMBCODE", "CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('GDEF00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""

	Dim MTableT1
	Dim MTableT2
	Set MTableT1=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET1=STACK","IO")
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SELECTED","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER","CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SERINO","CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "AMBCODE","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",13,3,"R","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_SF_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "FOKOD","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_VRI_VR_C","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_VRI_VR_R1","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_VRI_VR_R2","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_VRI_VR_R3","CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_VRI_NUM1","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SF_VRI_NUM2","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT1.Addfield2 "REZ_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""

	Set MTableT2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET2=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT2.Addfield2 "REZ_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT2.Setd7FieldEvent "SELECTED","ONVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SELECTED_OV"
	MTableT2.Setd7FieldEvent "REZ_MIKTAR","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "REZ_MIKTAR_AV"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par

	MTable.AMBCODE = "3"
	Call RezerveveStokDetaylariniGetir()
End Sub
Sub RezerveveStokDetaylariniGetir()
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	StokKodu = S40T.KOD
	if trim(StokKodu) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Call SiprisListesiniGetir()
	Call DetayStokListesiniGetir()
End Sub
Sub SiprisListesiniGetir()
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	MTableT1.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	Set S40T1 = Doc.Select2("dde34r55", "STOK40T1", "", "", -1)
	MTableT1.Empty()
	if S40T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	For i = 1 to S40T1.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Rezervasyonlar Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S40T1.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT1.AddRow()
		MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT1.SELECTED = "E"
		MTableT1.KOD = S40T1.KOD(i)
		MTableT1.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT1.KOD, "")
		MTableT1.LOTNUMBER = S40T1.LOTNUMBER(i)
		MTableT1.SERINO = S40T1.SERINO(i)
		MTableT1.AMBCODE = S40T1.AMBCODE(i)
		MTableT1.SF_MIKTAR = S40T1.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT1.SF_SF_UNIT = S40T1.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		MTableT1.REZ_MIKTAR = S40T1.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT1.FOKOD = S40T1.FOKOD(i)
		MTableT1.SF_VRI_VR_C = S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
		MTableT1.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
		MTableT1.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
		MTableT1.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)
		MTableT1.SF_VRI_NUM1 = S40T1.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
		MTableT1.SF_VRI_NUM2 = S40T1.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
		MTableT1.LOCATION1 = S40T1.LOCATION1(i)
		MTableT1.LOCATION2 = S40T1.LOCATION2(i)
		MTableT1.LOCATION3 = S40T1.LOCATION3(i)
		MTableT1.LOCATION4 = S40T1.LOCATION4(i)
	Next
	MTableT1.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT1.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub DetayStokListesiniGetir()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")

	QStr = "KOD='" & S40T.KOD & "'&ALLOC_SYSSTATUS=' '"
	if trim(MTable.AMBCODE) <> "" Then 
		QStr = QStr & "&AMBCODE='" & MTable.AMBCODE & "'"
	End if
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("dde34r55", "STOK01", QStr, "KOD", -1)
	MTableT2.Empty()
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Stok Listesi Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT2.AddRow()
		MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		MTableT2.SELECTED = "H"
		MTableT2.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
		MTableT2.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT2.KOD, "")
		MTableT2.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		MTableT2.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		MTableT2.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		MTableT2.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT2.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		MTableT2.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		MTableT2.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		MTableT2.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
	Next
	MTableT2.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT2.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = 0
End Sub
Sub RezervasyonlariKaydet()
	Set MTableT1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET1")
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set S40T1 = Doc.GetTableobject("STOK40T1")
	Set MT1 = Doc.Select2("dr44t67", "MAKROTABLET1", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	Set MT2 = Doc.Select2("dr44t67sd", "MAKROTABLET2", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)

	S40T1.EmptyFiltered
	For i = 1 to MT1.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S40T1.AddRow
		S40T1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S40T1.KOD = MT1.KOD(i)
		S40T1.AMBCODE = MT1.AMBCODE(i)
		S40T1.SF_MIKTAR = MT1.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S40T1.SF_SF_UNIT = MT1.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		S40T1.LOTNUMBER = MT1.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S40T1.SERINO = MT1.SERINO(i)
		S40T1.FOKOD = MT1.FOKOD(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_C = MT1.SF_VRI_VR_C(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MT1.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MT1.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MT1.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MT1.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MT1.SF_VRI_NUM2(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION1 = MT1.LOCATION1(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION2 = MT1.LOCATION2(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION3 = MT1.LOCATION3(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION4 = MT1.LOCATION4(i)
	Next

	For i = 1 to MT2.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S40T1.AddRow
		S40T1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S40T1.KOD = MT2.KOD(i)
		S40T1.AMBCODE = MT2.AMBCODE(i)
		S40T1.SF_MIKTAR = MT2.REZ_MIKTAR(i)
		S40T1.SF_SF_UNIT = MT2.SF_UNIT(i)
		S40T1.LOTNUMBER = MT2.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S40T1.SERINO = MT2.SERINO(i)
		S40T1.FOKOD = MT2.FOKOD(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_C = MT2.VRI_VR_C(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MT2.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MT2.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MT2.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MT2.VRI_NUM1(i)
		S40T1.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MT2.VRI_NUM2(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION1 = MT2.LOCATION1(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION2 = MT2.LOCATION2(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION3 = MT2.LOCATION3(i)
		S40T1.LOCATION4 = MT2.LOCATION4(i)
	Next

	Doc.Save_Voucher()
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
Sub SELECTED_OV()
	Set MTableT2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET2")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if MTableT2.SELECTED = "E" Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC + MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR
	Else
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC - MTableT2.REZ_MIKTAR
	End if
End Sub
Sub REZ_MIKTAR_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MT2 = Doc.Select2("ee4r56", "MAKROTABLET2", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = 0
		Exit Sub
	End if
	MT2.GroupBy("KOD")
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MT2.REZ_MIKTAR
End Sub

Makro Kodu : SAYIM-01
Hangi Tablo için : SY99PO
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :
