Kod : 
Dim S01Arr, S01Arr1, Tarih
Sub Makro1()
	S01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"SF_VRI_VR_RC", "SF_VRI_VR_R1", "SF_VRI_VR_R2", "SF_VRI_VR_R3", "SF_VRI_NUM1", "SF_VRI_NUM2", "SF_SF_UNIT")
	S01Arr1 = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"VRI_VR_RC", "VRI_VR_R1", "VRI_VR_R2", "VRI_VR_R3", "VRI_NUM1", "VRI_NUM2", "SF_UNIT")

	Call GirisCikisFisleriOlustur()
	'Call KodLotSeriTransferFisiOlustur()
	'Doc.d7MsgBox "Kod/Lot/Seri Transfer Fiþlerini oluþturmadan önce Süz butonuna tekrar basmayý unutmayýnýz", "Dikkat", MB_OK+MB_ICONWARNING
'	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_HESAPLA"
End Sub
Sub GirisCikisFisleriOlustur()
	if trim(SAYM99E.S_EVRAKNO1) <> "" Then SayimTarihi = Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("SAYM11E", "TARIH", SAYM99E.S_EVRAKNO1, ""))
	Tarih = InputBox("Envanter Tarihini Giriniz", "Freedom", SayimTarihi)
	if trim(Tarih) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set S99 = Doc.Select2("_S99", "SAYM99T", "", "KOD", -1)
	S99.GroupBy("KOD")
	Set S99G = Doc.Select2("_S99G", "_S99", "SF_FARK>0", "", -1)
	Set S99C = Doc.Select2("_S99C", "_S99", "SF_FARK<0", "", -1)

	If S99C.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		ret = CikisFisiniOlustur(S99C)
		if ret <> 0 Then 
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Çýkýþ fiþi kaydedilemedi. Fiþte gerekli düzeltmeleri yaptýktan sonra" & vbcrlf & "tekrar Süz butonuna basýn ve makroyu tekrar çalýþtýrýn", "Hata", MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		End If
	End If

	if S99G.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		ret = GirisFisiniOlustur(S99G)
	End if
	if ret = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem Baþarý ile tamamlandý.", "Freedom", MB_OK
	End if
End Sub
Function CikisFisiniOlustur(S99C)
	CikisFisiniOlustur = -1
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK21", "")
	Set S21E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21E")	
	Set S21T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK21T")	
	Doc1.New_Voucher()
	S21E.AMBCODE = SAYM99T.AMBCODE
	S21E.TARIH = Tarih
	For i = 1 to SAYM99T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & SAYM99T.GetRowCount()
		CikacakMiktar = Doc.DRound(SAYM99T.SF_FARK(i))
		if CikacakMiktar < 0 Then
			satirNo = S21T.AddRow
		 	S21T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S21T.KOD = SAYM99T.KOD(i)
			S21T.CIKAN_MIKTAR = Abs(CikacakMiktar)
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.RunCommand "HESAPLANMIS_RECETENIN_LOT_SERI_BUL"
	CikisFisiniOlustur = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if CikisFisiniOlustur = 0 Then
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		Doc1.RUN_APP_EXIT_ONIDLE
	End if
End Function
Function GirisFisiniOlustur(S99C)
	GirisFisiniOlustur = -1
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK24", "")
	Set S24E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK24E")	
	Set S24T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK24T")	
	Doc1.New_Voucher()
	S24E.AMBCODE = SAYM99T.AMBCODE
	S24E.TARIH = Tarih
	For i = 1 to SAYM99T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & SAYM99T.GetRowCount()
		GirenMiktar = Doc.DRound(SAYM99T.SF_FARK(i))
		if GirenMiktar > 0 Then
			satirNo = S24T.AddRow
		 	S24T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S24T.KOD = SAYM99T.KOD(i)
			S24T.GIREN_MIKTAR = Abs(GirenMiktar)
		End if
	Next
	GirisFisiniOlustur = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if GirisFisiniOlustur = 0 Then
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		Doc1.RUN_APP_EXIT_ONIDLE
	End if
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SAYIM-02

Dim S01Arr, OldS01Arr, NewS01Arr
Sub Makro1()
	S01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"SF_VRI_VR_RC", "SF_VRI_VR_R1", "SF_VRI_VR_R2", "SF_VRI_VR_R3", "SF_VRI_NUM1", "SF_VRI_NUM2", "SF_SF_UNIT", "AMBCODE")

	OldS01Arr = Array("KOD", "LOTNUMBER", "SERINO", "LOCATION1", "LOCATION2", "LOCATION3", "LOCATION4", "FOKOD", _
					"OSF_VRI_VR_C", "OSF_VRI_VR_R1", "OSF_VRI_VR_R2", "OSF_VRI_VR_R3", "OSF_VRI_NUM1", "OSF_VRI_NUM2", "OSF_SF_UNIT", "AMBCODE_TR")


	NewS01Arr = Array("NEWCODE", "NEWLOTNUMBER", "NEWSERINO", "NEWLOCATION1", "NEWLOCATION2", "NEWLOCATION3", "NEWLOCATION4", "NEWFOKOD", _
					"PN_SF_VRI_VR_C", "PN_SF_VRI_VR_R1", "PN_SF_VRI_VR_R2", "PN_SF_VRI_VR_R3", "PN_SF_VRI_NUM1", "PN_SF_VRI_NUM2", "OSF_SF_UNIT", "TARGETAMBCODE_TR")

	Call KodLotSeriTransferFisiOlustur()
End Sub
Sub KodLotSeriTransferFisiOlustur()
	Set S99LISTE = Doc.Select2("_S99L", "SAYM99T", "SF_MIKTAR_01>0&SF_FARK<>0", "KOD", -1)
	Set S99SONUC = Doc.Select2("_S99S", "SAYM99T", "SF_MIKTAR_11>0&SF_FARK<>0", "KOD", -1)
	if S99LISTE.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	Depo = SAYM99T.DEPO(1)
	if trim(Depo) = "" Then
		Set G00 = Doc.Select1("Fr33", "GDEF00", "MRPDEYOK10<>'1'", "", -1)
		Depo = G00.KOD(1)
	End if
	S25E.AMBCODE = Depo
	S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = Depo

	EskiKod = ""
	For i = 1 to S99LISTE.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Kod/Lot/Seri transfer fiþi yaratýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S99LISTE.GetRowCount()
		if S99LISTE.KOD(i) <> EskiKod Then
			Set SONUC = Doc.Select2("ddee", "_S99S", "KOD='" & S99LISTE.KOD(i) &"'", "", -1)
			DagitimSatir = 1
		End if
		ListeMiktar = S99LISTE.SF_MIKTAR_01(i)
		For j = DagitimSatir to SONUC.GetRowCount()
			Cikis = False
			SonucMiktar = SONUC.SF_MIKTAR_11(j)
			if SonucMiktar > ListeMiktar Then
				DagitilacakMiktar = ListeMiktar
				SONUC.SF_MIKTAR_11(j) = SONUC.SF_MIKTAR_11(j)  - DagitilacakMiktar

				Cikis = True
			Else
				DagitilacakMiktar = SonucMiktar
				ListeMiktar = ListeMiktar - SonucMiktar
				SONUC.SF_MIKTAR_11(j) = 0
				DagitimSatir = DagitimSatir + 1
				if ListeMiktar = 0 Then Cikis = True
			End if

			satirNo = S25T.AddRow
			S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			For k = 0 to uBound(S01Arr)
				S25TOldAlan = OldS01Arr(k)
				S01Alan = S01Arr(k)
				S25T.SetFieldValue S25TOldAlan, satirNo, S99LISTE.GetFieldValue(S01Alan,i)
			Next
			S25T.SF_MIKTAR = DagitilacakMiktar

			For k = 0 to uBound(S01Arr)
				S25TNewAlan = NewS01Arr(k)
				S01Alan = S01Arr(k)
				S25T.SetFieldValue S25TNewAlan, satirNo, SONUC.GetFieldValue(S01Alan,j)
			Next

			if Cikis Then Exit For
		Next
		EskiKod = S99LISTE.KOD(i)
	Next
	Call EtkisizSatirlariSil(S25T)
	'Call SendTableToExcel(S25T)
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
'	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub EtkisizSatirlariSil(ByRef S25T)
	For i = 1 to S25T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Etkisiz satýrlar siliniyor : " & i & " / " & S25T.GetRowCount()
		EskiDeger = ""
		YeniDeger = ""
		For k = 0 to uBound(OldS01Arr)
			S25TOldAlan = OldS01Arr(k)
			S25TNewAlan = NewS01Arr(k)
			EskiDeger = EskiDeger & S25T.GetFieldValue(S25TOldAlan, i)
			YeniDeger = YeniDeger & S25T.GetFieldValue(S25TNewAlan, i)
		Next
		if YeniDeger = EskiDeger Then
			S25T.KOD(i) = ""
			S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT(i) = ""
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName

	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		ncol = ncol+1
		if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
		AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
			Align = "RIGHT"
		Else
			Align = "LEFT"
		End if
		pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
		pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
			If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
				pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
			ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
				OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
			Else
				pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S

Hangi Tablo için : STOK40

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SIP", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Setd7FieldEvent "SELECTED","ONVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SELECTED_OV"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
	Call STOK01_Doldur()
End Sub
Sub STOK01_Doldur()
	Set S40E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("dde34r55", "STOK01", "ALLOC_SIPNO='" & S40E.EVRAKNO & "'&ALLOC_SYSSTATUS='2'", "", -1)
	Alanlar = MTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	MTableT.Empty()
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()		
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		For Each AlanAdi in Alanlar
			if AlanAdi <> "KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
				AlanDegeri = S01.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
				MTableT.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, -1, AlanDegeri
			End if
		Next
		MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD, "")
	Next
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
End Sub
Sub SevkEmriOlustur()
	Set S40E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set S40T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK40T")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	Set MT = Doc.Select2("sdf5r55yy7", "MAKROTABLET", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Satýr seçmediniz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if MTableT.SELECTED(i) = "E" Then
			Eklenen = Eklenen + 1
			if Eklenen = 1 Then
				Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK30", "")
				Set S30E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK30E")
				Set S30T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK30T")
				Doc1.New_Voucher
				S30E.AMBCODE = MTableT.AMBCODE(i)
				S30T.Empty
			End if
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & Eklenen
			satirNo = S30T.AddRow()
			S30T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S30T.CH_KOD = S40E.MUSTERIKODU
			S30T.CH_KOD_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", S40E.MUSTERIKODU, "")

			S30T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_USTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_USTATUS(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_NONMOVABL = MTableT.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)

			Set S40T1 = Doc.Select1("d4546gg", "STOK40T", "ARTNO='" & MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			S30T.SP_REV_RTESTARIH = S40T1.REV_RTESTARIH
			S30T.SP_SF_PMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_PMIKTAR
			S30T.SP_SF_IMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_IMIKTAR
			S30T.KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
			S30T.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD(i), "")
			S30T.LOTNUMBER = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
			S30T.SERINO = MTableT.SERINO(i)
			S30T.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S30T.SF_SF_UNIT = MTableT.SF_UNIT(i)
			S30T.LOCATION1 = MTableT.LOCATION1(i)
			S30T.LOCATION2 = MTableT.LOCATION2(i)
			S30T.LOCATION3 = MTableT.LOCATION3(i)
			S30T.LOCATION4 = MTableT.LOCATION4(i)
			S30T.FOKOD = MTableT.FOKOD(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_C = MTableT.VRI_VR_C(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R1(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R2(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R3(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MTableT.VRI_NUM1(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MTableT.VRI_NUM2(i)
			S30T.SF_AUTOCALC = "E"
			S30T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR	 = MTableT.STOK_MIKTAR(i)
			S30T.KOD_STOK00_IUNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", MTableT.KOD(i), "")
			S30T.SP_TOLERANS = S40T1.TOLERANS
			S30T.SP_TOLERANSTYPE = S40T1.TOLERANSTYPE
		End if
	Next
	if Eklenen > 0 Then Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Call STOK01_Doldur()
End Sub
Sub SELECTED_OV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if MTableT.SELECTED = "E" Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC + MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
	Else
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC - MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
	End if
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SEVKEMRI2
Hangi Tablo için : STOK30
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Sevk Emri Oluþtur
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "SIPNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "CHSIPNO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SIP", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_SEC", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SEVKEMRIOPT", "CHAR",1,0,"R","",""
	MTable.SEVKEMRIOPT = "0"

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SELECTED", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Setd7FieldEvent "SELECTED","ONVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SELECTED_OV"

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
End Sub
Sub STOK01_Doldur()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	SiparisNo = MTable.SIPNO
	if trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then
		Set S40E = Doc.Select1("fsf3f3f", "STOK40E", "CHSIPNO='" & MTable.CHSIPNO & "'", "", -1)
		if S40E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			SiparisNo = S40E.EVRAKNO(1)
		End if
	End if
	if trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("dde34r55", "STOK01", "ALLOC_SIPNO='" & SiparisNo & "'&ALLOC_SYSSTATUS='2'", "", -1)
	Alanlar = MTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	MTableT.Empty()
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()		
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Rezervasyonlar Getiriliyor : " & i & " / " & S01.GetRowCount()
		MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		For Each AlanAdi in Alanlar
			if AlanAdi <> "KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
				AlanDegeri = S01.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
				MTableT.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, -1, AlanDegeri
			End if
		Next
		MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD, "")
	Next
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
End Sub
Sub SevkEmriOlustur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")

	Set MT = Doc.Select2("sdf5r55yy7", "MAKROTABLET", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Satýr seçmediniz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set S30E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK30E")
	Set S30T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK30T")

	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		if MTableT.SELECTED(i) = "E" Then
			Eklenen = Eklenen + 1
			if Eklenen = 1 and trim(MTable.SEVKEMRIOPT) = "0" Then
				Doc.New_Voucher
				S30E.AMBCODE = MTableT.AMBCODE(1)
				S30T.Empty
			End if
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & Eklenen
			satirNo = S30T.AddRow()
			S30T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S30T.CH_KOD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i), "")
			S30T.CH_KOD_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", S30T.CH_KOD, "")

			S30T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_USTATUS = MTableT.ALLOC_USTATUS(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = MTableT.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = MTableT.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
			S30T.OALLOC_NONMOVABL = MTableT.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)

			Set S40T1 = Doc.Select1("d4546gg", "STOK40T", "ARTNO='" & MTableT.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			S30T.SP_REV_RTESTARIH = S40T1.REV_RTESTARIH
			S30T.SP_SF_PMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_PMIKTAR
			S30T.SP_SF_IMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_IMIKTAR
			S30T.KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
			S30T.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD(i), "")
			S30T.LOTNUMBER = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
			S30T.SERINO = MTableT.SERINO(i)
			S30T.SF_MIKTAR = MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S30T.SF_SF_UNIT = MTableT.SF_UNIT(i)
			S30T.LOCATION1 = MTableT.LOCATION1(i)
			S30T.LOCATION2 = MTableT.LOCATION2(i)
			S30T.LOCATION3 = MTableT.LOCATION3(i)
			S30T.LOCATION4 = MTableT.LOCATION4(i)
			S30T.FOKOD = MTableT.FOKOD(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_C = MTableT.VRI_VR_C(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R1(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R2(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MTableT.VRI_VR_R3(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MTableT.VRI_NUM1(i)
			S30T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = MTableT.VRI_NUM2(i)
			S30T.SF_AUTOCALC = "E"
			S30T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR	 = MTableT.STOK_MIKTAR(i)
			S30T.KOD_STOK00_IUNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", MTableT.KOD(i), "")
			S30T.SP_TOLERANS = S40T1.TOLERANS
			S30T.SP_TOLERANSTYPE = S40T1.TOLERANSTYPE
		End if
	Next
	if Eklenen > 0 Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
	'Call STOK01_Doldur()
End Sub
Sub SELECTED_OV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	if MTableT.SELECTED = "E" Then
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC + MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
	Else
		MTable.TOPLAM_SEC = MTable.TOPLAM_SEC - MTableT.SF_MIKTAR
	End if
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SEVKEMRI3
Hangi Tablo için : STOK66
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Çeki Listesini Sevk Emrine Dönüþtür
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Call SevkEmriOlustur()
End Sub
Sub SevkEmriOlustur()
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("wef3fsdf3wfwsd", "STOK01", "ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE='CL'&ALLOC_EVRAKNO='" & STOK66E.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu Çeki Listesi daha önce sevk emrine dönüþtürülmüþ", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK30", "")
	Set S30E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK30E")
	Set S30T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK30T")
	
	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S30E.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(1)
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i
		satirNo = S30T.AddRow()
		S30T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S30T.CH_KOD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E", "MUSTERIKODU", S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i), "")
		S30T.CH_KOD_CARI00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", S30T.CH_KOD, "")
		
		S30T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_USTATUS = S01.ALLOC_USTATUS(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
		S30T.OALLOC_NONMOVABL = S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(i)
		
		Set S40T1 = Doc.Select1("d4546gg", "STOK40T", "ARTNO='" & S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
		S30T.SP_REV_RTESTARIH = S40T1.REV_RTESTARIH
		S30T.SP_SF_PMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_PMIKTAR
		S30T.SP_SF_IMIKTAR = S40T1.SF_IMIKTAR
		S30T.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
'		S30T.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S01.KOD(i), "")
		S30T.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S30T.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		S30T.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S30T.SF_SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		S30T.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		S30T.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		S30T.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		S30T.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		S30T.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		S30T.SF_VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S30T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		S30T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		S30T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		S30T.SF_AUTOCALC = "E"
		S30T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR  = S01.STOK_MIKTAR(i)
		S30T.KOD_STOK00_IUNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", S01.KOD(i), "")
		S30T.SP_TOLERANS = S40T1.TOLERANS
		S30T.SP_TOLERANSTYPE = S40T1.TOLERANSTYPE
	Next
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(S30E.EVRAKNO) & " no.lu Sevk Emri yaratýldý", "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
	Else
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Sevk Emri kaydedilemedi", "Ýþlem Baþarýsýz", MB_OK
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SIL
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Liste Sayfasýndaki MPS 'leri Toplu Sil
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to LISTET.GetRowCount()
		Doc.Load_Voucher LISTET.EVRAKNO(i)
		Doc.Delete_Voucher()
	Next
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý
Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : 
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : 
Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : 
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : ST00KTA
Hangi Tablo için : STOK001
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Toplu KTA giriþi
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "ARANACAKDEYIM","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.ARANACAKDEYIM=""

	AnaTabloStr = "LISTET"
	Set AnaTablo = Doc.GetTableObject("LISTET")
	AnaTablo.SafePack()
	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STACK","IO")

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	Call AddAllFields(MTableT)
	Call AddUDEFFields(MTableT)

	Call AlanlariDoldur(AnaTablo, MTableT)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	EkranFormu = GetEkranFormu(AnaTabloStr, AnaTablo)
'	Call MsgBoxToTxt("", EkranFormu)
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",EkranFormu, Par
End Sub
Function GetEkranFormu(AnaTabloStr, AnaTablo)
	'EnBoy = GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
	'En = EnBoy(0)
	'Boy = EnBoy(1)
	'if En = 0 or Boy = 0 Then
		En = 1000
		Boy = 690
	'Else
	'	En = En - 24
	'	Boy = Boy - 60
	'End if 
	GridEn = En - 25
	GridBoy = Boy - 90

	FormStr = "FORM MAKROFORMU1 HEIGHT=" & Boy & "P WIDTH=" & En & "P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET CAPTION= " & Chr(34) & "Sipariþ Seviyeli MPS Yaratma" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "MENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   POPUP TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Dosya" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   MENUITEM TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Ýþlem Yapmadan Çý&kýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL KEY=ESCAPE" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   ENDPOPUP" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDMENUDEF" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=10p" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "//LTEXT LEFT=1 TOP=+0 T='Ünvanda Geçen Deyim : '" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "//EDITTEXT LEFT=E+10 TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE.ARANACAKDEYIM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "GRID LEFT=1 TOP=+0 HEIGHT=" & GridBoy & "p WIDTH=" & GridEn & "p TABLE=MAKROTABLET HEADERROWHEIGHT=35P" & vbCrLf

	MainTableStr = GetMainTableStr()
	FormStr = FormStr & MainTableStr & vbCrLf

	UDFStr = GetUDFStr()
	FormStr = FormStr & UDFStr & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDGRID" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=3 TOP=E+7p WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "KTA 'larý Kaydet" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','KTA_Yaz')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
'	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=30 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Açýlacak MPS 'leri Hesapla" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','MPSHesapla')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
'	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "MPS 'leri Yarat" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','MPSOlustur')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Çýkýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDFORM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = Replace(FormStr, "@@@BUMAKRO@@@", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME)

	GetEkranFormu = FormStr
End Function
Function GetUDFStr()
	UDFStr = ""
	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='STOK00'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = "UDEFF__" & trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		Else
			UDFStr = UDFStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
		End if
	Next
	GetUDFStr = UDFStr
End Function
Sub AddUDEFFields(MTableT)
	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='STOK00'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to U10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = "UDEFF__" & trim(U10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = U10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Aciklama = trim(U10T.VARASPNAME(i))
		AlanTuru = trim(U10T.VARTYPE(i))
		Grupkodu = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))
		Tablo = trim(U10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT4(i))
		Uzunluk = U10T.VARLEN(i)
		Ondalik = U10T.VARSIG(i)

		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",Grupkodu
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","F2=DO_BRW('" & Tablo & "')",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",""
		Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
			MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DOUBLE",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"R","",""
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub AddAllFields(MTableT)
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "LISTET")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		if AlanAdi = "KOD" or AlanAdi = "AD" Then
			Aciklama = Grid(i,2)
			AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
			Grupkodu = ""
			Tablo = ""
			Uzunluk = Grid(i,5)
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			if AlanTuru = "D" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DATE",10,0,"L","",""
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",Grupkodu
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Tablo) <> "" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","F2=DO_BRW('" & Tablo & "')",""
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "CHAR",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"L","",""
			Elseif AlanTuru = "N" Then
				MTableT.Addfield2 AlanAdi, "DOUBLE",Uzunluk,Ondalik,"R","",""
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function GetMainTableStr()
	MainTableStr = ""
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", "LISTET")
	For i = 0 to uBound(Grid, 1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		if AlanAdi = "KOD" or AlanAdi = "AD" Then
			Aciklama = Replace(Grid(i,2), "\n", " ")
			Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(13), " ")
			Aciklama = Replace(Aciklama, chr(10), " ")
			AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
			Grupkodu = ""
			Tablo = ""
			if AlanTuru = "D" Then
				MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
			Elseif AlanTuru = "C" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
				MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "COMBOBOX D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
			Else
				MainTableStr = MainTableStr & "EDITTEXT D=" & AlanAdi & " CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & Aciklama & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
			End if
		End if
	Next
	GetMainTableStr = MainTableStr
End Function
Sub MsgBoxToTxt(MesajBaslik, Mesaj)
	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	tmpFolder = fso.GetSpecialFolder(2)
	dosyaAdi = tmpFolder & "\Hata.txt"
	Set HataFile = Fso.OpenTextFile(dosyaAdi, 2, True)
	HataFile.WriteLine(MesajBaslik & " : " & vbcrlf & Mesaj)
	HataFile.Close
	Set wshShell = CreateObject("WSCript.shell")
	wshshell.run "notepad " & dosyaAdi, 1, True
	set wshshell = nothing
End Sub
Sub AlanlariDoldur(AnaTablo, MainTableT)
	MainTableT.Empty
	Alanlar1 = MainTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to AnaTablo.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MainTableT.AddRow()
		For each Alan in Alanlar1
			if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") > 0 or Alan = "KOD" or Alan = "AD" Then
				if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") = 0 Then
					AlanDegeri = LISTET.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
				Else
					AlanAdiIndex = GetAlanAdiIndex(Mid(Alan, 8), "STOK00")
					KTA_Alan = AlanAdiIndex(0)
					KTA_Index = AlanAdiIndex(1)

					AlanDegeri = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", LISTET.KOD(i), KTA_Alan, KTA_Index)
				End if
				MainTableT.SetFieldValue Alan, satirNo, AlanDegeri
			End if
		Next
	Next
End Sub
Sub KTA_Yaz()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	Set AnaTablo = Doc.GetTableObject("LISTET")
	Alanlar = MTableT.GetFieldNames("")
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001", "STOK00", MTableT.KOD(i))
		Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")   
		For each Alan in Alanlar
			if InStr(1,Alan, "UDEFF__") > 0 Then
				KTA_Alan = Mid(Alan, 8)
				AlanDegeri = MTableT.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
				K = T_U1.SetFieldValue(KTA_Alan,-1,AlanDegeri)
				T_U1.Exec "SAVE" 
			End if
		Next 
	Next
End Sub
Function GetAlanAdiIndex(AlanAdi, KTA_Tablo)
	AlanAdi = trim(AlanAdi)
	Dim AlanArr(1)
	AlanArr(0) = AlanAdi
	AlanArr(1) = 0

	GetAlanAdiIndex = AlanArr
	tire = Instr(1,AlanAdi, "_")
	If tire = 0 Then Exit Function

	If Right(AlanAdi,1) =  "_" Then Exit Function
	If Right(AlanAdi,1) < "0" or Right(AlanAdi,1) > "9" Then Exit Function

	Set U10T = Doc.Select1("dd3333", "UFLD10T", "EVRAKNO='STOK00'&VARCODE='" & AlanAdi & "'", "", -1)
	if U10T.GetRowCount() > 0 and Len(AlanAdi) <= Len(U10T.VARCODE) Then Exit Function

	Uzunluk = Len(AlanAdi)
	if Uzunluk < 3 Then Exit Function
	For i = Uzunluk-1 to Uzunluk-3 step -1
		chr1 = Mid(AlanAdi, i, 1)
		if chr1 = "_" Then
			AlanArr(0) = Left(AlanAdi, i-1)
			AlanArr(1) = Doc.CDbl(Mid(AlanAdi, i+1))
		End if
	Next
	GetAlanAdiIndex = AlanArr
End Function
Function GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
	Dim ScrArr(1)
	ScrArr(0) = 0
	ScrArr(1) = 0

	Set IE = CreateObject("InternetExplorer.Application")
	IE.Visible = False
	IE.Navigate ("about:blank")
	Set Scr = IE.Document.parentWindow.screen
	Width = Scr.Width
	Height = Scr.Height
	AvailableWidth = Scr.availwidth
	AvailableHeight = Scr.availheight
	BitsPerPixel = Scr.colordepth

	ScrArr(0) = AvailableWidth
	ScrArr(1) = AvailableHeight

	Set Scr = Nothing
	IE.Quit
	Set IE = Nothing
	GetScreenWidthAndHeight = ScrArr
End Function

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý
Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : 
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : 
Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : 
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : TALEP
Hangi Tablo için : STOK46
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Sipariþ Talep Baðlantýlarýný Yap
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	TalepStr = InputBox("Talep numarasýný giriniz" & vbcrlf & " Birden çok talep için virgülle ayýrýnýz")
	if trim(TalepStr) = "" Then Exit Sub
	
	STOK46T.SafePack()

	TalepArr = Split(TalepStr, ",")
	QStr = "SELECT * FROM STOK47T "
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE ACIKKAPALI=' ' AND ("
	s = 0
	For Each talep in TalepArr
		s = s + 1
		if s > 1 Then QStr = QStr & " OR"
		QStr = QStr & " EVRAKNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(Talep), 10) & "'"
	Next
	QStr = QStr & ") ORDER BY KOD, RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH"
	Doc.RunSQLQuery "xxx", QStr
	Set S47T = Doc.GetTableObject("xxx")

	Set S46T = Doc.Select2("wef23fdsfsdf", "STOK46T", "", "SRNUM+REV_RTESTARIH", -1)
	For i = 1 to S46T.GetRowCount()
		srNum = Doc.CDbl(S46T.SRNUM(i))
		STOK46T.SetCurrentRow(srNum)

		Set S46T1 = Doc.Select2G("wef23fsdf", "STOK46T1", "", "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
		if S46T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Call TalepBagla(S47T, srNum, STOK46T.SF_MIKTAR)
		Else
			if S46T1.SF_MIKTAR < STOK46T.SF_MIKTAR Then
				Call TalepBagla(S47T, srNum, STOK46T.SF_MIKTAR - S46T1.SF_MIKTAR)
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub TalepBagla(S47T, sipSatirNo, BaglanacakMiktar)
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Doc.CreateSObject("Parameters")

	Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_NAME_001",STOK46T.GetAppName()	
	Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_CURENT_ROW",sipSatirNo
	Doc.RunCommand1 "CMD_SET_CURRENT_ROW",Par

	STOK46T1.SafePack()

	For i = 1 to S47T.GetRowCount()
		TalepBakiye = S47T.SF_MIKTAR(i) - S47T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(i)
		if S47T.KOD(i) = STOK46T.KOD and TalepBakiye > 0 Then
			if TalepBakiye < BaglanacakMiktar Then
				BaglananMiktar = TalepBakiye
			Else
				BaglananMiktar = BaglanacakMiktar
			End if
			satirNo = STOK46T1.AddRow		

'			Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_NAME_001",STOK46T1.GetAppName()	
'			Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_CURENT_ROW",satirNo
'			Doc.RunCommand1 "CMD_SET_CURRENT_ROW",Par

			if STOK46T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
				satirNo = STOK46T1.AddRow()
			Else
				STOK46T1.InsertRow(1)
				satirNo = 1
			End if
			STOK46T1.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

			STOK46T1.KOD = STOK46T.KOD
			STOK46T1.TALEPNO = S47T.EVRAKNO(i)
			STOK46T1.TALEPARTNO = S47T.ARTNO(i)
			STOK46T1.SF_MIKTAR = BaglananMiktar
			STOK46T1.SF_SF_UNIT = S47T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
			S47T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(i) = S47T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK(i) + BaglananMiktar
			BaglanacakMiktar = BaglanacakMiktar - BaglananMiktar
			if BaglanacakMiktar = 0 Then Exit For
		End if
	Next
	if BaglananMiktar > 0 Then STOK46T1.Sort("TALEPARTNO_STOK47T_RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH")
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Doc.Save_Voucher()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("TEKL40", "")
	Set T40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("TEKL40E")
	Set T40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("TEKL40T")
	
	Doc1.New_Voucher
	T40E.MUSTERIKODU = TEKL20E.BASE_DF_CARIHESAP
	T40E.TEKLIF_FIYAT_PB = TEKL20E.TEKLIF_FIYAT_PB
	T40E.INCO_KOD = TEKL20E.INCO_KOD
	T40E.CU_LME = TEKL20E.CU_LME
	T40E.CO_PB = TEKL20E.CU_PB
	TeslimTarihi = InputBox("Teslim Tarihini Giriniz", "", Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))

	For i = 1 to TEKL20TI.GetRowCount()
		if trim(TEKL20TI.KAYNAKTYPE(i)) = "M" Then
			TEKL20T2.SafePack
			TEKL20TI.SetCurrentRow(i)
			Set T20T2 = Doc.Select2("dwfw444", "TEKL20T2", "", "", -1)
			if T20T2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
				satirNo = T40T.AddRow()
				T40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				T40T.KOD = TEKL20TI.KOD(i)
				T40T.SF_MIKTAR = TEKL20TI.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				T40T.OR_FIYAT = TEKL20TI.FIYAT(i)
				T40T.PRICEUNIT = TEKL20TI.PRICEUNIT(i)
				T40T.RTESTARIH = TeslimTarihi
				T40T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_3 = TEKL20E.EVRAKNO & TEKL20TI.TRNUM(i)
			Else
				For j = 1 to T20T2.GetRowCount()
					satirNo = T40T.AddRow()
					T40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
					T40T.KOD = TEKL20TI.KOD(i)
					T40T.SF_PMIKTAR = T20T2.SF_PAKETSAYISI(j)
					T40T.SF_IMIKTAR = T20T2.SF_PAKETICERIGI(j)
'					T40T.SF_MIKTAR = TEKL20TI.SF_MIKTAR(i)
					T40T.OR_FIYAT = TEKL20TI.FIYAT(i)
					T40T.PRICEUNIT = TEKL20TI.PRICEUNIT(i)
					T40T.RTESTARIH = TeslimTarihi
					T40T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_3 = TEKL20E.EVRAKNO & TEKL20TI.TRNUM(i)
					PaketBirimi = GetPaketBirimi(T20T2.AMBALAJ_TURU(j))
					T40T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2 = PaketBirimi
				Next
			End if
		End if
	Next
'	if trim(T40E.MUSTERIKODU) <> "" Then
		ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
		
		if ret = 0 Then
			nret = Doc1.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("xxx_UFLD", "TEKL40E", T40E.EVRAKNO)
			Set KTA_Table = Doc1.GetTableObject("xxx_UFLD")
			KTA_Table.ANALIZEVNO = TEKL20E.EVRAKNO
			KTA_Table.Exec "SAVE"

			nret = Doc.UFLD00_CreateEditTable("xxx_UFLD", "TEKL20E", TEKL20E.EVRAKNO)
			Set KTA_Table = Doc.GetTableObject("xxx_UFLD")
			KTA_Table.TEKLIFNO = T40E.EVRAKNO
			KTA_Table.Exec "SAVE"
		End if
'	End if
End Sub
Function GetPaketBirimi(AmbalajTuru)
	if trim(AmbalajTuru) = "7" Then
		GetPaketBirimi = "KANGAL"
	Elseif trim(AmbalajTuru) = "5" Then
		GetPaketBirimi = "PALET"
	Else
		GetPaketBirimi = "MAKARA"
	End if
End Function

	TFile.WriteLine "3.  Delivery        :  To be discussed before order confirmation."

	TFile.WriteLine "     is an option, which belongs to Surtel."
	TFile.WriteLine "5.  Packing         :  Packed on strong non-returnable wooden/plywood drums."
	TFile.WriteLine "6.  Tolerance       :  (+/-) 5 % per individual drum length and on total quantity"
	TFile.WriteLine "7.  Force Majeure   :  The delivery shall be subject to standart force majeure conditions."
	TFile.WriteLine "8.  Cancellation    :  Orders cannot be cancelled and postponed."
	TFile.WriteLine "9.  Confirmation    :  Subject to our final confirmation."
	TFile.WriteLine ""
	TFile.WriteLine "     The prices are variable on LME (CU) " & trim(T40E.CO_PB) & " " & T40E.CU_LME & "/MTon. The final price to be calculated after the order confirmation."
	TFile.WriteLine "                             PRICE VARIATION FORMULA"
	TFile.WriteLine "                             QP ((BLME-CLME)*VF))= CP"
	TFile.WriteLine "                             QP     = QUATED PRICE PER KM IN " & trim(T40E.CO_PB)
	TFile.WriteLine "                             BLME = BASE LME IN " & trim(T40E.CO_PB)
	TFile.WriteLine "                             CLME = CURRENT LME IN " & trim(T40E.CO_PB)

	TFile.WriteLine Space(80) & "Swift code : YAPITRIS815"

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : TOLERANS
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Ýþ Emri Miktarlarýný Tolerans Kadar Arttýr (Ýþ Emri Planlama Paneli Ýçin)
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set f = doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Doc.CreateSObject("Parameters")

	For i = 1 to M22_MMPS10E_LIST.GetRowCount()

		Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_NAME_001",M22_MMPS10E_LIST.GetAppName()	
		Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_CURENT_ROW",i
		Doc.RunCommand1 "CMD_SET_CURRENT_ROW",Par

		For j = 1 to M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.GetRowCount()

			Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_NAME_001",M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.GetAppName()	
			Par.SetParameter "USR_TABLE_CURENT_ROW",j
			Doc.RunCommand1 "CMD_SET_CURRENT_ROW",Par

			ToleransTuru = M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.EVRAKNO_MMPS10E_TOLERANSTYPE
			if trim(ToleransTuru) = "+" or trim(ToleransTuru) = "-+" Then
				Bakiye = M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_MIKTART
				if Doc.Is_EY1(M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_TRANSFER_DIKKATE_ALINSIN) Then
					Bakiye = Bakiye - M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_HM_NET_TRANSFER
				End if
				Tolerans = M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.EVRAKNO_MMPS10E_TOLERANS
				M22_MMPS10T_SUB_LIST.R_HM_BAKIYE = Bakiye * (1 + Tolerans / 100)
			End if
		Next
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set M10TM = Doc.Select1("weff4ff", "MMPS10TM", "ACIK_KAPALI=''&MUSTERIKODU='1S0010'", "", -1)
	if M10TM.GetRowCOunt() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	STOK51T.Empty
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
	Set M10TM1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	For i = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK51T.AddRow()
		STOK51T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		STOK51T.KOD = M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i)
		STOK51T.OLD50_SF_PMIKTAR = M10TM.SF_PAKETSAYISI(i)
		STOK51T.OLD50_SF_IMIKTAR = M10TM.SF_PAKETICERIGI(i)
		STOK51T.RIMALATISTEKTARIH = M10TM.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(i)
		STOK51T.TOLERANSTYPE = M10TM.TOLERANSTYPE(i)
		STOK51T.TOLERANS = M10TM.TOLERANS(i)
		Doc1.Load_Voucher M10TM.EVRAKNO(i)
		For j = 1 to M10TM1.GetRowCount()
			if M10TM1.TRNUM(j) = M10TM.TRNUM(i) Then
				M10TM1.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE(j) = "PC"
				M10TM1.KAYNAKMPS(j) = STOK51E.EVRAKNO
				M10TM1.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM(j) = STOK51T.TRNUM
				M10TM1.MUSTERISIPINO(j) = ""
				M10TM1.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(j) = ""
				M10TM1.MUSTERIKODU(j) = ""
			End if
		Next
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	'MTable.Addfield2 "ARANACAKDEYIM","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	'MTable.ARANACAKDEYIM=""

	Dim MTableT
	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLESYM=SAYM11T(NOFILE)","IO")
	'MTableT.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('BOMU01T','=BOMREC_OPERASYON','BOMREC_CODE+AKTIF_PASIF','BRW_BOMU01T_0',,""BOMREC_CODE==MAKROTABLESYM.KOD&&AKTIF_PASIF=='*'&&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE=='I'"")",""
'	MTableT.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('BOMU01T','=BOMREC_OPERASYON','BOMREC_CODE+AKTIF_PASIF','BRW_BOMU01T_0',,""BOMREC_CODE==MAKROTABLESYM.KOD&&AKTIF_PASIF=='*'&&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE=='I'"",""BOMREC_CODE==MAKROTABLESYM.KOD&&AKTIF_PASIF=='*'"",""BOMREC_CODE==MAKROTABLESYM.KOD&&AKTIF_PASIF=='*'"",""BOMREC_CODE==MAKROTABLESYM.KOD&&AKTIF_PASIF=='*'"")",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('IMLT01')",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "ISNO","CHAR",17,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Setd7FieldEvent "OPERASYON","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "OPERASYON_AV"
	MTableT.Setd7FieldEvent "ISNO","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "ISNO_AV"
			
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par

	Call FillMTableT()
'	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0

End Sub
Sub FillMTableT()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GettableObject("MAKROTABLESYM")
	MTableT.Empty()

	Set S11T = Doc.GetTableObject("SAYM11T")
	Alanlar = S11T.GetFieldNames("")

	For i = 1 to S11T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = MTableT.AddRow
		For each alan in Alanlar
			alanDegeri = S11T.GetFieldValue(Alan, i)
			MTableT.SetFieldValue alan, satirNo, alanDegeri
		Next
		MTableT.SF_VRI_VR_R3(satirNo) = ""
		MTableT.OPERASYON(satirNo) = S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)
	Next
End Sub
Sub Parcala()
	Set MSYM = Doc.Select2("e23fwfsdf", "MAKROTABLESYM", "", "", -1)
	Set S11E = Doc.GetTableObject("SAYM11E")
	EvrakNo = S11E.EVRAKNO
	Call Kaydet()
	Doc.Save_Voucher
	Doc.New_Voucher
	S11E.NOTES = "YM SAYIM PARÇALAMA LÝSTESÝ Liste No : " & trim(EvrakNo)
	For i = 1 to MSYM.GetRowCount()
		if trim(MSYM.OPERASYON(i)) <> "" Then
			Call ParcalaVeEkle(i)
		Else
			Call Aktar(i)
		End if
	Next
	Doc.Save_Voucher
End Sub
Sub ParcalaVeEkle(sSatirNo)
	Set MSYM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESYM")
	Set S11T = Doc.GetTableObject("SAYM11T")
	S11T.SafePack()

	Set oPar = Doc.CreateSObject("Parameters")
	oPar.SetParameter "SAYIMRECETESI","1"
	oPar.SetParameter "SAYIMOPERASYONU", MSYM.OPERASYON(sSatirNo)
 	ret=Doc.Calc_Bom_Par("1", "M", MSYM.KOD(sSatirNo), 1, MSYM.SF_MIKTAR(sSatirNo),"","","","","","","","", oPar)

	Set Tbmrsys =Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT")
	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		if Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			satirNo = S11T.AddRow
			S11T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S11T.KOD = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)
			S11T.SF_MIKTAR = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
			S11T.AMBCODE = MSYM.AMBCODE(sSatirNo)
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Sub Aktar(sSatirNo)
	Set MSYM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESYM")
	Set S11T = Doc.GetTableObject("SAYM11T")
	satirNo = S11T.AddRow

	S11T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	S11T.KOD = MSYM.KOD(sSatirNo)
	S11T.LOTNUMBER = MSYM.LOTNUMBER(sSatirNo)
	S11T.SERINO = MSYM.SERINO(sSatirNo)
	S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MSYM.SF_VRI_VR_R1(sSatirNo)
	S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MSYM.SF_VRI_VR_R2(sSatirNo)
	S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MSYM.SF_VRI_VR_R3(sSatirNo)
	S11T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MSYM.SF_VRI_NUM1(sSatirNo)

	S11T.SF_MIKTAR = MSYM.SF_MIKTAR(sSatirNo)
	S11T.AMBCODE = MSYM.AMBCODE(sSatirNo)
End Sub
Sub Kaydet()
	Set MSYM = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESYM")
	Set S11T = Doc.GetTableObject("SAYM11T")

	S11T.Empty
	For i = 1 to MSYM.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S11T.AddRow
		S11T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S11T.KOD = MSYM.KOD(i)
		S11T.LOTNUMBER = MSYM.LOTNUMBER(i)
		S11T.SERINO = MSYM.SERINO(i)
		S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = MSYM.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
		S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = MSYM.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
		S11T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = MSYM.OPERASYON(i)
		S11T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = MSYM.SF_VRI_NUM1(i)

		S11T.SF_MIKTAR = MSYM.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S11T.AMBCODE = MSYM.AMBCODE(i)
	Next
End Sub
Sub OPERASYON_AV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESYM")
	if trim(MTableT.OPERASYON) = "" or trim(MTableT.KOD) = "" Then Exit Sub

	QStr = "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&BOMREC_CODE='" & MTableT.KOD & "'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='I'"

	Set B01T = Doc.Select1("de4t678u", "BOMU01T", QStr, "", -1)
	Set B01T2 = Doc.Select2("der4tthhh77", B01T.GetAppName, "BOMREC_OPERASYON='" & MTableT.OPERASYON & "'", "", -1)
	if B01T2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		For i = 1 to B01T.GetRowCount()
			OpStr = OpStr & trim(B01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON(i)) & " - " & trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", B01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON(i), "")) & vbcrlf
		Next
		InputStr = trim(MTableT.OPERASYON) & " operasyonu bu ürünün ürün aðacýnda bulunamadý" & vbcrlf & _
						"Aþaðýdaki Operasyonlar 'dan birini giriniz : " & vbcrlf & OpStr
		Op = InputBox(InputStr)
		MTableT.OPERASYON = Op
	End if
End Sub
Sub ISNO_AV()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLESYM")
	if trim(MTableT.ISNO) = "" Then Exit Sub
	IsNo = Replace(MTableT.ISNO, " ", "")
	Bolu = InStr(1,IsNo,"/")
	if Bolu <= 1 or Bolu = Len(IsNo) Then Exit Sub

	IsNo = Doc.RightJustify(Left(IsNo, Bolu-1) & Right("000000" & Mid(IsNo, Bolu+1), 6), 16)

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("wdf3dfgdg", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & IsNo & "'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	if trim(M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE) <> "I" Then Exit Sub

	MTableT.KOD = M10T.R_MAMULKODU
	MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT.KOD, "")
	MTableT.SF_SF_UNIT = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", MTableT.KOD, "")
	Set M10T1 = Doc.Select1("wssdf3dfgdg", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & M10T.EVRAKNO & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'&SRNUM>'" & M10T.SRNUM & "'", "", -1)
	if M10T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		MTableT.OPERASYON = M10T.R_OPERASYON
	End if
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : CABLE / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : _GNPRG1

Kod : 
Dim Q10T
Sub Makro1()
	Dim MTable
	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')=",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')=",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER","CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAH_KODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('IMLT00')=",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAH_ADI","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON","CHAR",6,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('IMLT01')=",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OPERASYON_ADI","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SERINO","CHAR",20,0,"L","",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "MPSNO","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "MPSNO_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "KOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "KOD_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "LOTNUMBER","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "LOTNUMBER_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "TEZGAH_KODU","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "TEZGAH_KODU_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "OPERASYON","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "OPERASYON_AV"

End Sub
Sub MPSNO_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MTable.KOD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "MAMULSTOKKODU", MTable.MPSNO, "")
	Call KOD_AV()
End Sub
Sub KOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MTable.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTable.KOD, "")
End Sub
Sub TEZGAH_KODU_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MTable.TEZGAH_ADI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "AD", MTable.TEZGAH_KODU, "")
End Sub
Sub OPERASYON_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	MTable.OPERASYON_ADI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", MTable.OPERASYON, "")
End Sub
Sub LOTNUMBER_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set S31E = Doc.Select1("aefa3fae", "SFDC31E", "LOTNUMBER='" & MTable.LOTNUMBER & "'", "", -1)
	if S31E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		MTable.KOD = S31E.STOK_CODE
		Call KOD_AV()

		MTable.TEZGAH_KODU = S31E.X_T_ISMERKEZI
		Call TEZGAH_KODU_AV()

		MTable.OPERASYON = S31E.OPERASYON
		Call OPERASYON_AV()
	End if
End Sub
Function GFLSMik_Round(Sayi)
	GFLS_Mik = Doc.Customp_Gets("GFLS_MIK")
	Nokta = InStr(1, GFLS_Mik, ".")
	Ondalik = Doc.CDbl(trim(Mid(GFLS_Mik, Nokta + 1)))
	GFLSMik_Round = Doc.Round(Sayi, Ondalik)
End Function
Sub KaliteTestSonuclariGirisi()
	Set MTable1=Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_KALITE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "KOD","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER","CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SERINO","CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	
	Set MTable1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	QVLCODE1 = Doc.Customp_Gets("QVLCODE1")
	if InStr(1, QVLCODE1, "K") > 0 Then
		StokKodu = MTable1.KOD
	End if
	if InStr(1, QVLCODE1, "L") > 0 Then
		LotNo = MTable1.LOTNUMBER
	End if
	if InStr(1, QVLCODE1, "S") > 0 Then
		SeriNo = MTable1.SERINO
	End if

	Set par = Doc.CreateSObject("Parameters")
	par.SetParameter "CREATE_QVAL_DEF_TABLE","E" 

	par.SetParameter "ICODE", MTable1.TEZGAH_KODU 
	par.SetParameter "OCODE", MTable1.OPERASYON 

	ret = Doc.QVAL01E_CREATEEDITTABLE("___QVAL___",StokKodu, LotNo, SeriNo, par)
	Set KaliteTable = Doc.GetTableObject("___QVAL___")

'	TblStr = 	par.GetParameter("_QVAL_DEF_TABLE")
'msgbox TblStr
	TblStr = "_QVAL_DEF_TABLE_110234"
	Set Q10T = Doc.GetTableObject(TblStr)

'	Call AlanAdlariniveEventleriEkle(Q10T, KaliteTable)

	MTable.KOD = MTable1.KOD
	MTable.LOTNUMBER = MTable1.LOTNUMBER
	MTable.SERINO = MTable1.SERINO
	
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	EkranFormu = GetEkranFormu(Q10T, KaliteTable)
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "KALITE",EkranFormu, Par
End Sub
Sub KaliteYaz()
	Set KaliteTable = Doc.GetTableObject("___QVAL___")
	ret=Doc.SetSaveable(TRUE)
	KaliteTable.Exec "Save"
	ret=Doc.SetSaveable(FALSE)
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Kalite parametreleri kaydedildi", "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
Function GetEkranFormu(Q10T, KaliteTable)
'	EnBoy = GetScreenWidthAndHeight()
'	En = EnBoy(0)
'	Boy = EnBoy(1)
	En = 0
	if En = 0 or Boy = 0 Then
		En = 1024
		Boy = 690
	Else
		En = En - 25
		Boy = Boy - 60
	End if 
	TabEn = En - 5
	TabBoy = Boy - 80

	GridEn = En - 5
	GridBoy = TabBoy - 65

	FormStr = "FORM KALITE HEIGHT=" & Boy & "P WIDTH=" & En & "P SCROLLBARS=NO CAPTIONTYPE=SYS" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET CAPTION= " & Chr(34) & "Kalite" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "MENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   POPUP TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Dosya" & Chr(34) & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   MENUITEM TEXT=" & Chr(34) & "Ýþlem Yapmadan Çý&kýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL KEY=ESCAPE" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "   ENDPOPUP" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDMENUDEF" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SET FIXEDPITCH_EDIT=1" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "DEFINEFONT ID=""SABIT"" FACE=""Courier New"" POINT=10 //BOLD=Y " & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "DEFINEFONT ID=""BOLD"" FACE=""MS Sans Serif"" POINT=8 BOLD=Y " & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "LOCATE TOP=10p" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "GROUPBOX TEXT="""" AUTOSIZE=N LEFT=35 TOP=+0 WIDTH=300p HEIGHT=80p BORDER=BUMP" & vbCrLf
	
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=+5p TOP=+10p WIDTH=80p FONT=""BOLD"" T='Stok Kodu'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SAVELOCATION XCCCT" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 FONT=""BOLD"" T=':'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE_KALITE.KOD" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "RESTORELOCATION XCCCT" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=+0 TOP=E+3p WIDTH=80p FONT=""BOLD"" T='Lot No'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SAVELOCATION XCCCT" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 FONT=""BOLD"" T=':'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE_KALITE.LOTNUMBER" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "RESTORELOCATION XCCCT" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=+0 TOP=E+3p WIDTH=80p FONT=""BOLD"" T='Seri No'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 FONT=""BOLD"" T=':'" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 D=MAKROTABLE_KALITE.SERINO" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDGROUPBOX" & vbCrLf
	FormStr = FormStr & "SAVELOCATION XCCCX" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=80 TOP=15p WIDTH=15 HEIGHT=30p CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Kaydet" & Chr(34) & " ID=" & Chr(34) & "MT_RUNMACRO_AFTERCALC('MAKR7S.@@@BUMAKRO@@@','KaliteYaz')" & chr(34) & vbcrlf
	FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=+0 TOP=E+5p WIDTH=15 HEIGHT=30p CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Çýkýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbcrlf


	FormStr = FormStr & "RESTORELOCATION XCCCX" & vbCrLf

	QValStr = GetQValStr(Q10T, KaliteTable)
	FormStr = FormStr & QValStr & vbCrLf
	'FormStr = FormStr & "BUTTON LEFT=E+2 TOP=+0 WIDTH=20 HEIGHT=2 CAPTION=" & Chr(34) & "Çýkýþ" & Chr(34) & " ID=ID_APP_CANCEL" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = FormStr & "ENDFORM" & vbCrLf

	FormStr = Replace(FormStr, "@@@BUMAKRO@@@", SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME)

	GetEkranFormu = FormStr
End Function
Function GetQValStr(Q10T, KaliteTable)
	Ust = 4
	IkinciBlok = 11
'	QValStr = "LOCATE TOP=" & Ust & vbCrLf
 	QValStr = "FRAME AUTOSIZE=N LEFT=1P TOP=E+5p WIDTH=1010p HEIGHT=500P HORIZONTALSCROLLBAR=NO BORDER=0" & vbcrlf
	For i = 1 to Q10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = trim(Q10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = Q10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if

		Aciklama1 = Q10T.VARASPNAME(i)

		GrupAciklama = Doc.GetGecoValue("QVALSC", "AD", Q10T.VARCODE(i), -1)

		Aciklama = GrupAciklama
		AlanTuru = trim(Q10T.VARTYPE(i))
		AlanGenisligi = Q10T.VARLEN(i)
		Grupkodu = trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT3(i))

'		if i >= IkinciBlok Then Left1 = 68 Else Left1 = 1
'		if i = IkinciBlok Then QValStr = QValStr & "LOCATE TOP=" & Ust & vbCrLf
		Left1 = 1
		QValStr = QValStr & "RTEXT LEFT=" & Left1 & " TOP=E+0 T='" & Aciklama & "' FONT=""SABIT""" & vbCrLf
		if AlanTuru = "D" Then
			QValStr = QValStr & "COMBOBOX "
		Elseif AlanTuru = "T" and trim(Grupkodu) <> "" Then
			QValStr = QValStr & "COMBOBOX "
		Else
			QValStr = QValStr & "EDITTEXT "
		End if
		QValStr = QValStr & "LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 "
		if AlanGenisligi > 50 Then QValStr = QValStr & "WIDTH=50"
		QValStr = QValStr & "D=" & KaliteTable.GetAppName & "." & AlanAdi & vbCrLf
		MinMax = ""
		if trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1(i)) <> "" Then
			MinMax = "Min.:" & trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1(i))
		End if
		if trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2(i)) <> "" Then
			if trim(MinMax) <> "" Then MinMax = MinMax & " - "
			MinMax = MinMax & "Max.:" & trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2(i))
		End if
		if trim(MinMax) <> "" Then
			QValStr = QValStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+3p TOP=+0 T='" & MinMax & "' FONT=""SABIT""" & vbCrLf
		End if

		if trim(GrupAciklama) <> trim(Aciklama1) Then
			QValStr = QValStr & "LTEXT LEFT=E+5p TOP=+0 T='(" & trim(Aciklama1) & ")' FONT=""SABIT""" & vbCrLf
			Aciklama = Aciklama & " - " & trim(Aciklama1)
		End if


	Next
 	QValStr = QValStr & "ENDFRAME"
	GetQValStr = QValStr
End Function
Sub FIELD_AV()
	Set KaliteTable = Doc.GetTableObject("___QVAL___")
	AlanAdi = Doc.GetGUIControl_Field()
	Set Q10T1 = Doc.Select2("wef23fsdf", Q10T.GetAppName, "VERIFIKASYONTEXT4='"& AlanAdi & "'", "", -1)
	if Q10T1.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
		if trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1) = "" and trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2) = "" Then Exit Sub
		
		AltLimit = -999999999999999999999999999999999999999999
		UstLimit = 9999999999999999999999999999999999999999999

		if trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1) <> "" Then AltLimit = Doc.Cdbl(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1)
		if trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2) <> "" Then UstLimit = Doc.Cdbl(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2)
	
		Deger = KaliteTable.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, -1)
		if Deger >= AltLimit and Deger <= UstLimit Then Exit Sub

		if trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1) <> "" and trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2) <> "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Girilen Deðer " & AltLimit & " ile " & UstLimit & " arasýnda olmalýdýr", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Elseif trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1) <> "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Girilen Deðer " & AltLimit & " den büyük veya eþit olmalýdýr", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		Elseif trim(Q10T1.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2) <> "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Girilen Deðer " & UstLimit & " den küçük veya eþit olmalýdýr", "Uyarý", MB_OK
		End if	
	End if
End Sub
Sub AlanAdlariniveEventleriEkle(Q10T, KaliteTable)
	For i = 1 to Q10T.GetRowCount()
		AlanAdi = trim(Q10T.VARCODE(i))
		AlanIndex = Q10T.VARINDEX(i)
		if trim(AlanIndex) <> "" Then
			AlanAdi = AlanAdi & "_" & trim(AlanIndex)
		End if
		Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT4(i) = AlanAdi

		if trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT1(i)) <> "" or trim(Q10T.VERIFIKASYONTEXT2(i)) <> "" Then
			KaliteTable.Setd7FieldEvent AlanAdi,"AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "FIELD_AV"
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Doc.LoadFirst_Voucher()
	Do
		Set S00 = Doc.Select1("dd222", "STOK00", "KOD='" & BOMU01E.MAMULCODE & "'", "", -1)
		NetsisKodu = S00.SUPPLIERCODE
		if trim(NetsisKodu) <> "" Then
			BOMU01E.ACIKLAMA = NetsisKodu
			Doc.Save_Voucher
		End if
	Loop While Doc.LoadNext_Voucher = 0
End Sub
